===== /crates/rustynet-backend-wireguard/src/in_memory.rs =====

   1: use std::collections::{BTreeMap, VecDeque};
   2: use std::net::SocketAddr;
   3: use std::process::Command;
   4: use std::time::Duration;
   5: 
   6: use base64::prelude::*;
   7: use rustynet_backend_api::{
   8:     AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, AuthoritativeTransportResponse, BackendCapabilities,
   9:     BackendError, ExitMode, NodeId, PeerConfig, Route, RuntimeContext, SocketEndpoint,
  10:     TunnelBackend, TunnelStats,
  11: };
  12: 
  13: const WG_LATEST_HANDSHAKES_MAX_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024;
  14: 
  15: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  16: pub enum RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind {
  17:     RoundTrip,
  18:     Send,
  19: }
  20: 
  21: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  22: pub struct RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation {
  23:     pub kind: RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind,
  24:     pub local_addr: SocketAddr,
  25:     pub remote_addr: SocketAddr,
  26:     pub payload: Vec<u8>,
  27:     pub timeout: Option<Duration>,
  28: }
  29: 
  30: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  31: struct InMemoryAuthoritativeTransport {
  32:     identity: AuthoritativeTransportIdentity,
  33:     scripted_round_trip_results: VecDeque<ScriptedAuthoritativeRoundTrip>,
  34:     scripted_send_results: VecDeque<Result<(), BackendError>>,
  35:     recorded_operations: Vec<RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation>,
  36: }
  37: 
  38: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  39: enum ScriptedAuthoritativeRoundTrip {
  40:     Static(Result<AuthoritativeTransportResponse, BackendError>),
  41:     StunMappedEndpoint {
  42:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
  43:         mapped_endpoint: SocketAddr,
  44:     },
  45: }
  46: 
  47: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  48: pub struct WireguardBackend {
  49:     running: bool,
  50:     context: Option<RuntimeContext>,
  51:     peers: BTreeMap<NodeId, PeerConfig>,
  52:     peer_latest_handshakes_by_node: BTreeMap<NodeId, u64>,
  53:     peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint: BTreeMap<String, u64>,
  54:     routes: Vec<Route>,
  55:     exit_mode: ExitMode,
  56:     stats: TunnelStats,
  57:     authoritative_transport: Option<InMemoryAuthoritativeTransport>,
  58: }
  59: 
  60: impl Default for WireguardBackend {
  61:     fn default() -> Self {
  62:         Self {
  63:             running: false,
  64:             context: None,
  65:             peers: BTreeMap::new(),
  66:             peer_latest_handshakes_by_node: BTreeMap::new(),
  67:             peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint: BTreeMap::new(),
  68:             routes: Vec::new(),
  69:             exit_mode: ExitMode::Off,
  70:             stats: TunnelStats::default(),
  71:             authoritative_transport: None,
  72:         }
  73:     }
  74: }
  75: 
  76: impl WireguardBackend {
  77:     fn endpoint_cache_key(endpoint: SocketEndpoint) -> String {
  78:         format!("{}:{}", endpoint.addr, endpoint.port)
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     fn ensure_running(&self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  82:         if self.running {
  83:             return Ok(());
  84:         }
  85: 
  86:         Err(BackendError::not_running(
  87:             "wireguard backend is not running",
  88:         ))
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     fn refresh_stats(&mut self) {
  92:         self.stats.peer_count = self.peers.len();
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     fn fetch_latest_handshakes(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  96:         let interface_name = match &self.context {
  97:             Some(ctx) => &ctx.interface_name,
  98:             None => {
  99:                 return Err(BackendError::not_running(
 100:                     "wireguard backend context missing",
 101:                 ));
 102:             }
 103:         };
 104: 
 105:         let output = Command::new("wg")
 106:             .arg("show")
 107:             .arg(interface_name)
 108:             .arg("latest-handshakes")
 109:             .output()
 110:             .map_err(|err| BackendError::internal(format!("wg show failed: {err}")))?;
 111: 
 112:         if !output.status.success() {
 113:             let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
 114:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 115:                 "wg show failed with status {}: {stderr}",
 116:                 output.status
 117:             )));
 118:         }
 119: 
 120:         let stdout = output.stdout;
 121:         if stdout.len() > WG_LATEST_HANDSHAKES_MAX_BYTES {
 122:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 123:                 "wg latest-handshakes output exceeded {WG_LATEST_HANDSHAKES_MAX_BYTES} bytes"
 124:             )));
 125:         }
 126: 
 127:         let output_str = String::from_utf8(stdout)
 128:             .map_err(|err| BackendError::internal(format!("wg show output not utf8: {err}")))?;
 129: 
 130:         let mut pubkey_to_node_id = BTreeMap::new();
 131:         for (node_id, peer) in &self.peers {
 132:             pubkey_to_node_id.insert(peer.public_key, node_id.clone());
 133:         }
 134: 
 135:         for line in output_str.lines() {
 136:             let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
 137:             if parts.len() < 2 {
 138:                 continue;
 139:             }
 140:             let pubkey_str = parts[0];
 141:             let timestamp_str = parts[1];
 142: 
 143:             let timestamp = timestamp_str.parse::<u64>().map_err(|err| {
 144:                 BackendError::internal(format!(
 145:                     "wg latest-handshakes timestamp parse failed: {err}"
 146:                 ))
 147:             })?;
 148: 
 149:             if timestamp == 0 {
 150:                 continue;
 151:             }
 152: 
 153:             let pubkey_vec = BASE64_STANDARD.decode(pubkey_str).map_err(|err| {
 154:                 BackendError::internal(format!("wg pubkey base64 decode failed: {err}"))
 155:             })?;
 156: 
 157:             let pubkey: [u8; 32] = pubkey_vec.try_into().map_err(|vec: Vec<u8>| {
 158:                 BackendError::internal(format!("wg pubkey wrong length: {}", vec.len()))
 159:             })?;
 160: 
 161:             if let Some(node_id) = pubkey_to_node_id.get(&pubkey) {
 162:                 self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node
 163:                     .insert(node_id.clone(), timestamp);
 164:             }
 165:         }
 166: 
 167:         Ok(())
 168:     }
 169: 
 170:     #[doc(hidden)]
 171:     pub fn set_peer_latest_handshake_unix_for_test(
 172:         &mut self,
 173:         node_id: &NodeId,
 174:         latest_handshake_unix: Option<u64>,
 175:     ) {
 176:         if let Some(timestamp) = latest_handshake_unix {
 177:             self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node
 178:                 .insert(node_id.clone(), timestamp);
 179:         } else {
 180:             self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node.remove(node_id);
 181:         }
 182:         if let Some(peer) = self.peers.get(node_id) {
 183:             let endpoint_key = Self::endpoint_cache_key(peer.endpoint);
 184:             if let Some(timestamp) = latest_handshake_unix {
 185:                 self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint
 186:                     .insert(endpoint_key, timestamp);
 187:             } else {
 188:                 self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint
 189:                     .remove(&endpoint_key);
 190:             }
 191:         }
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     #[doc(hidden)]
 195:     pub fn cached_peer_latest_handshake_unix_for_test(
 196:         &self,
 197:         node_id: &NodeId,
 198:     ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 199:         self.ensure_running()?;
 200:         if !self.peers.contains_key(node_id) {
 201:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 202:         }
 203:         Ok(self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node.get(node_id).copied())
 204:     }
 205: 
 206:     #[doc(hidden)]
 207:     pub fn set_endpoint_latest_handshake_unix_for_test(
 208:         &mut self,
 209:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 210:         latest_handshake_unix: Option<u64>,
 211:     ) {
 212:         let mut target_node_id = None;
 213:         for (node_id, peer) in &self.peers {
 214:             if peer.endpoint == endpoint {
 215:                 target_node_id = Some(node_id.clone());
 216:                 break;
 217:             }
 218:         }
 219: 
 220:         if let Some(node_id) = target_node_id {
 221:             if let Some(timestamp) = latest_handshake_unix {
 222:                 self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node
 223:                     .insert(node_id, timestamp);
 224:             } else {
 225:                 self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node.remove(&node_id);
 226:             }
 227:         }
 228:         let endpoint_key = Self::endpoint_cache_key(endpoint);
 229:         if let Some(timestamp) = latest_handshake_unix {
 230:             self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint
 231:                 .insert(endpoint_key, timestamp);
 232:         } else {
 233:             self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint
 234:                 .remove(&endpoint_key);
 235:         }
 236:     }
 237: 
 238:     fn authoritative_transport_unavailable_error(&self, action: &str) -> BackendError {
 239:         BackendError::internal(format!(
 240:             "authoritative shared transport {action} unavailable: {}",
 241:             self.transport_socket_identity_blocker().unwrap_or_else(|| {
 242:                 "in-memory backend authoritative shared transport is not configured".to_string()
 243:             })
 244:         ))
 245:     }
 246: 
 247:     #[doc(hidden)]
 248:     pub fn configure_authoritative_shared_transport_for_test(
 249:         &mut self,
 250:         local_addr: SocketAddr,
 251:         label: impl Into<String>,
 252:     ) {
 253:         self.authoritative_transport = Some(InMemoryAuthoritativeTransport {
 254:             identity: AuthoritativeTransportIdentity {
 255:                 local_addr,
 256:                 label: label.into(),
 257:             },

 260:             recorded_operations: Vec::new(),
 261:         });
 262:     }

 274:         if self.authoritative_transport.is_none() {
 275:             let local_addr = SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 51_820));

 352: 
 353:     fn build_scripted_stun_response(
 354:         request: &[u8],
 355:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 356:         mapped_endpoint: SocketAddr,
 357:         local_addr: SocketAddr,
 358:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportResponse, BackendError> {
 359:         const STUN_MAGIC_COOKIE: u32 = 0x2112A442;
 360:         const STUN_BINDING_RESPONSE: u16 = 0x0101;
 361:         const STUN_ATTR_XOR_MAPPED_ADDRESS: u16 = 0x0020;
 362: 
 363:         if request.len() < 20 {
 364:             return Err(BackendError::internal(
 365:                 "scripted STUN round trip request too short",
 366:             ));
 367:         }
 368:         let tx_id = &request[8..20];
 369:         let family = match mapped_endpoint {
 370:             SocketAddr::V4(_) => 0x01u8,
 371:             SocketAddr::V6(_) => 0x02u8,
 372:         };
 373:         let mut attribute = Vec::with_capacity(24);
 374:         attribute.extend_from_slice(&STUN_ATTR_XOR_MAPPED_ADDRESS.to_be_bytes());
 375:         match mapped_endpoint {
 376:             SocketAddr::V4(endpoint) => {
 377:                 attribute.extend_from_slice(&(8u16).to_be_bytes());
 378:                 attribute.push(0);
 379:                 attribute.push(family);
 380:                 let port = endpoint.port() ^ ((STUN_MAGIC_COOKIE >> 16) as u16);
 381:                 attribute.extend_from_slice(&port.to_be_bytes());
 382:                 let cookie = STUN_MAGIC_COOKIE.to_be_bytes();
 383:                 for (byte, mask) in endpoint.ip().octets().iter().zip(cookie.iter()) {
 384:                     attribute.push(byte ^ mask);
 385:                 }
 386:             }
 387:             SocketAddr::V6(endpoint) => {
 388:                 attribute.extend_from_slice(&(20u16).to_be_bytes());
 389:                 attribute.push(0);
 390:                 attribute.push(family);
 391:                 let port = endpoint.port() ^ ((STUN_MAGIC_COOKIE >> 16) as u16);
 392:                 attribute.extend_from_slice(&port.to_be_bytes());
 393:                 let cookie = STUN_MAGIC_COOKIE.to_be_bytes();
 394:                 for (index, byte) in endpoint.ip().octets().iter().enumerate() {
 395:                     let mask = if index < 4 {
 396:                         cookie[index]
 397:                     } else {
 398:                         tx_id[index - 4]
 399:                     };
 400:                     attribute.push(byte ^ mask);
 401:                 }
 402:             }
 403:         }
 404: 
 405:         let mut payload = Vec::with_capacity(20 + attribute.len());
 406:         payload.extend_from_slice(&STUN_BINDING_RESPONSE.to_be_bytes());
 407:         payload.extend_from_slice(&(attribute.len() as u16).to_be_bytes());
 408:         payload.extend_from_slice(&STUN_MAGIC_COOKIE.to_be_bytes());
 409:         payload.extend_from_slice(tx_id);
 410:         payload.extend_from_slice(&attribute);
 411:         Ok(AuthoritativeTransportResponse {
 412:             local_addr,
 413:             remote_addr,
 414:             payload,
 415:         })
 416:     }
 417: }
 418: 
 419: impl TunnelBackend for WireguardBackend {
 420:     fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
 421:         "wireguard"
 422:     }
 423: 
 424:     fn capabilities(&self) -> BackendCapabilities {
 425:         BackendCapabilities {
 426:             supports_roaming: true,
 427:             supports_exit_nodes: true,
 428:             supports_lan_routes: true,
 429:             supports_ipv6: true,
 430:         }
 431:     }
 432: 
 433:     fn start(&mut self, context: RuntimeContext) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 434:         if self.running {
 435:             return Err(BackendError::already_running(
 436:                 "wireguard backend already started",
 437:             ));
 438:         }
 439: 
 440:         self.context = Some(context);
 441:         self.running = true;
 442:         self.refresh_stats();
 443:         Ok(())
 444:     }
 445: 
 446:     fn configure_peer(&mut self, peer: PeerConfig) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 447:         self.ensure_running()?;
 448:         let endpoint_key = Self::endpoint_cache_key(peer.endpoint);
 449:         if let Some(timestamp) = self
 450:             .peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint
 451:             .get(&endpoint_key)
 452:             .copied()
 453:         {
 454:             self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node
 455:                 .insert(peer.node_id.clone(), timestamp);
 456:         }
 457:         self.peers.insert(peer.node_id.clone(), peer);
 458:         self.refresh_stats();
 459:         Ok(())
 460:     }
 461: 
 462:     fn update_peer_endpoint(
 463:         &mut self,
 464:         node_id: &NodeId,
 465:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 466:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 467:         self.ensure_running()?;
 468:         let Some(peer) = self.peers.get_mut(node_id) else {
 469:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 470:         };
 471:         peer.endpoint = endpoint;
 472:         let endpoint_key = Self::endpoint_cache_key(endpoint);
 473:         if let Some(timestamp) = self
 474:             .peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint
 475:             .get(&endpoint_key)
 476:             .copied()
 477:         {
 478:             self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node
 479:                 .insert(node_id.clone(), timestamp);
 480:         }
 481:         Ok(())
 482:     }
 483: 
 484:     fn current_peer_endpoint(
 485:         &self,
 486:         node_id: &NodeId,
 487:     ) -> Result<Option<SocketEndpoint>, BackendError> {
 488:         self.ensure_running()?;
 489:         Ok(self.peers.get(node_id).map(|peer| peer.endpoint))
 490:     }
 491: 
 492:     fn peer_latest_handshake_unix(
 493:         &mut self,
 494:         node_id: &NodeId,
 495:     ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 496:         self.ensure_running()?;
 497:         if !self.peers.contains_key(node_id) {
 498:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 499:         }
 500: 
 501:         self.fetch_latest_handshakes()?;
 502: 
 503:         Ok(self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node.get(node_id).copied())
 504:     }
 505: 
 506:     fn remove_peer(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 507:         self.ensure_running()?;
 508:         if let Some(peer) = self.peers.remove(node_id) {
 509:             let endpoint_key = Self::endpoint_cache_key(peer.endpoint);
 510:             self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint
 511:                 .remove(&endpoint_key);
 512:         }
 513:         self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node.remove(node_id);
 514:         self.refresh_stats();
 515:         Ok(())
 516:     }
 517: 
 518:     fn apply_routes(&mut self, routes: Vec<Route>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 519:         self.ensure_running()?;
 520:         self.routes = routes;
 521:         Ok(())
 522:     }
 523: 
 524:     fn set_exit_mode(&mut self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 525:         self.ensure_running()?;
 526:         self.exit_mode = mode;
 527:         Ok(())
 528:     }
 529: 
 530:     fn stats(&self) -> Result<TunnelStats, BackendError> {
 531:         self.ensure_running()?;
 532:         Ok(self.stats)
 533:     }
 534: 
 535:     fn authoritative_transport_identity(&self) -> Option<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity> {
 536:         self.authoritative_transport
 537:             .as_ref()
 538:             .map(|transport| transport.identity.clone())
 539:     }
 540: 
 541:     fn authoritative_transport_round_trip(
 542:         &mut self,
 543:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 544:         payload: &[u8],
 545:         timeout: Duration,
 546:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportResponse, BackendError> {
 547:         self.ensure_running()?;
 548:         let Some(transport) = self.authoritative_transport.as_mut() else {
 549:             return Err(self.authoritative_transport_unavailable_error("round trip"));
 550:         };
 551:         let identity = transport.identity.clone();
 552:         transport
 553:             .recorded_operations
 554:             .push(RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation {
 555:                 kind: RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::RoundTrip,
 556:                 local_addr: identity.local_addr,
 557:                 remote_addr,
 558:                 payload: payload.to_vec(),
 559:                 timeout: Some(timeout),
 560:             });
 561:         let result = transport
 562:             .scripted_round_trip_results
 563:             .pop_front()
 564:             .ok_or_else(|| {
 565:                 BackendError::internal(
 566:                     "authoritative shared transport round trip has no scripted response",
 567:                 )
 568:             })?;
 569:         let result = match result {
 570:             ScriptedAuthoritativeRoundTrip::Static(result) => result?,
 571:             ScriptedAuthoritativeRoundTrip::StunMappedEndpoint {
 572:                 remote_addr: scripted_remote_addr,
 573:                 mapped_endpoint,
 574:             } => Self::build_scripted_stun_response(
 575:                 payload,
 576:                 scripted_remote_addr,
 577:                 mapped_endpoint,
 578:                 identity.local_addr,
 579:             )?,
 580:         };
 581:         if result.local_addr != identity.local_addr {
 582:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 583:                 "authoritative shared transport round trip returned local addr {} but backend identity is {}",
 584:                 result.local_addr, identity.local_addr
 585:             )));
 586:         }
 587:         Ok(result)
 588:     }
 589: 
 590:     fn authoritative_transport_send(
 591:         &mut self,
 592:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 593:         payload: &[u8],
 594:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, BackendError> {
 595:         self.ensure_running()?;
 596:         let Some(transport) = self.authoritative_transport.as_mut() else {
 597:             return Err(self.authoritative_transport_unavailable_error("send"));
 598:         };
 599:         let identity = transport.identity.clone();
 600:         transport
 601:             .recorded_operations
 602:             .push(RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation {
 603:                 kind: RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::Send,
 604:                 local_addr: identity.local_addr,
 605:                 remote_addr,
 606:                 payload: payload.to_vec(),
 607:                 timeout: None,
 608:             });
 609:         if let Some(result) = transport.scripted_send_results.pop_front() {
 610:             result?;
 611:         }
 612:         Ok(identity)
 613:     }
 614: 
 615:     fn shutdown(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 616:         self.ensure_running()?;
 617:         self.running = false;
 618:         self.context = None;
 619:         self.peers.clear();
 620:         self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_node.clear();
 621:         self.peer_latest_handshakes_by_endpoint.clear();
 622:         self.routes.clear();
 623:         self.exit_mode = ExitMode::Off;
 624:         self.stats = TunnelStats::default();
 625:         self.authoritative_transport = None;
 626:         Ok(())
 627:     }
 628: }

 678:             }])
 679:             .expect("route apply should succeed");

 688:     fn in_memory_backend_authoritative_transport_round_trip_records_shared_identity() {
 689:         let mut backend = WireguardBackend::default();
 690:         backend
 691:             .start(runtime_context())
 692:             .expect("backend should start");
 693:         let local_addr: SocketAddr = "0.0.0.0:51820".parse().unwrap();
 694:         let remote_addr: SocketAddr = "198.51.100.1:3478".parse().unwrap();
 695:         backend.configure_authoritative_shared_transport_for_test(
 696:             local_addr,
 697:             "wireguard-in-memory-authoritative-shared-transport",
 698:         );
 699:         backend.script_authoritative_round_trip_for_test(Ok(AuthoritativeTransportResponse {
 700:             local_addr,
 701:             remote_addr,
 702:             payload: b"response".to_vec(),
 703:         }));
 704: 
 705:         let response = backend
 706:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(remote_addr, b"request", Duration::from_millis(250))

 708: 
 709:         assert_eq!(response.local_addr, local_addr);
 710:         let operations = backend.recorded_authoritative_transport_operations_for_test();

 712:         assert_eq!(
 713:             operations[0].kind,
 714:             RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::RoundTrip
 715:         );
 716:         assert_eq!(operations[0].local_addr, local_addr);
 717:         assert_eq!(operations[0].remote_addr, remote_addr);
 718:     }

===== /crates/rustynet-backend-wireguard/src/linux_command.rs =====

   1: use std::collections::BTreeMap;
   2: use std::process::Command;
   3: 
   4: use rustynet_backend_api::{
   5:     BackendCapabilities, BackendError, BackendErrorKind, ExitMode, NodeId, PeerConfig, Route,
   6:     RuntimeContext, SocketEndpoint, TunnelBackend, TunnelStats,
   7: };
   8: 
   9: pub(crate) const WG_LATEST_HANDSHAKES_MAX_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024;
  10: 
  11: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  12: pub struct WireguardCommandOutput {
  13:     pub stdout: String,
  14:     pub stderr: String,
  15: }
  16: 
  17: pub trait WireguardCommandRunner {
  18:     fn run(&mut self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<(), BackendError>;
  19: 
  20:     fn run_capture(
  21:         &mut self,
  22:         program: &str,
  23:         args: &[String],
  24:     ) -> Result<WireguardCommandOutput, BackendError>;
  25: }
  26: 
  27: #[derive(Debug, Default)]
  28: pub struct LinuxCommandRunner;
  29: 
  30: impl WireguardCommandRunner for LinuxCommandRunner {
  31:     fn run(&mut self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  32:         let _ = self.run_capture(program, args)?;
  33:         Ok(())
  34:     }
  35: 
  36:     fn run_capture(
  37:         &mut self,
  38:         program: &str,
  39:         args: &[String],
  40:     ) -> Result<WireguardCommandOutput, BackendError> {
  41:         let output = Command::new(program)
  42:             .args(args)
  43:             .output()
  44:             .map_err(|err| BackendError::internal(format!("{program} spawn failed: {err}")))?;
  45:         if !output.status.success() {
  46:             let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).trim().to_string();
  47:             if stderr.is_empty() {
  48:                 return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
  49:                     "{program} exited with status {}",
  50:                     output.status
  51:                 )));
  52:             }
  53:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
  54:                 "{program} exited with status {}: {stderr}",
  55:                 output.status
  56:             )));
  57:         }
  58:         let stdout = String::from_utf8(output.stdout)
  59:             .map_err(|_| BackendError::internal(format!("{program} produced non-utf8 stdout")))?;
  60:         let stderr = String::from_utf8(output.stderr)
  61:             .map_err(|_| BackendError::internal(format!("{program} produced non-utf8 stderr")))?;
  62:         Ok(WireguardCommandOutput { stdout, stderr })
  63:     }
  64: }
  65: 
  66: #[derive(Debug)]
  67: pub struct LinuxWireguardBackend<R: WireguardCommandRunner> {
  68:     pub(crate) runner: R,
  69:     interface_name: String,
  70:     private_key_path: String,
  71:     listen_port: u16,
  72:     running: bool,
  73:     peers: BTreeMap<NodeId, PeerConfig>,
  74:     routes: Vec<Route>,
  75:     context: Option<RuntimeContext>,
  76:     exit_mode: ExitMode,
  77: }
  78: 
  79: impl<R: WireguardCommandRunner> LinuxWireguardBackend<R> {
  80:     pub fn new(
  81:         runner: R,
  82:         interface_name: impl Into<String>,
  83:         private_key_path: impl Into<String>,
  84:         listen_port: u16,
  85:     ) -> Result<Self, BackendError> {
  86:         let interface_name = interface_name.into();
  87:         let private_key_path = private_key_path.into();
  88:         validate_interface_name(&interface_name)?;
  89:         validate_private_key_path(&private_key_path)?;
  90:         validate_listen_port(listen_port)?;
  91:         Ok(Self {
  92:             runner,
  93:             interface_name,
  94:             private_key_path,
  95:             listen_port,
  96:             running: false,
  97:             peers: BTreeMap::new(),
  98:             routes: Vec::new(),
  99:             context: None,
 100:             exit_mode: ExitMode::Off,
 101:         })
 102:     }
 103: 
 104:     fn ensure_running(&self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 105:         if self.running {
 106:             return Ok(());
 107:         }
 108:         Err(BackendError::not_running(
 109:             "linux wireguard backend is not running",
 110:         ))
 111:     }
 112: 
 113:     pub(crate) fn ensure_cidr(value: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 114:         if value.is_empty() || !value.contains('/') {
 115:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr value"));
 116:         }
 117:         if !value
 118:             .chars()
 119:             .all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || ch == '.' || ch == ':' || ch == '/')
 120:         {
 121:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 122:                 "cidr contains invalid characters",
 123:             ));
 124:         }
 125:         Ok(())
 126:     }
 127: 
 128:     fn is_missing_ip_route_error(err: &BackendError) -> bool {
 129:         if err.kind != BackendErrorKind::Internal {
 130:             return false;
 131:         }
 132:         let message = err.message.to_ascii_lowercase();
 133:         message.contains("rtnetlink answers: no such process")
 134:             || message.contains("no such process")
 135:             || message.contains("no such file or directory")
 136:     }
 137: 
 138:     fn configure_interface(&mut self, context: &RuntimeContext) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 139:         Self::ensure_cidr(&context.local_cidr)?;
 140:         let add_args = [
 141:             "link".to_string(),
 142:             "add".to_string(),
 143:             "dev".to_string(),
 144:             self.interface_name.clone(),
 145:             "type".to_string(),
 146:             "wireguard".to_string(),
 147:         ];
 148:         if self.runner.run("ip", &add_args).is_err() {
 149:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
 150:             self.runner.run("ip", &add_args)?;
 151:         }
 152:         if let Err(err) = self.runner.run(
 153:             "wg",
 154:             &[
 155:                 "set".to_string(),
 156:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 157:                 "private-key".to_string(),
 158:                 self.private_key_path.clone(),
 159:                 "listen-port".to_string(),
 160:                 self.listen_port.to_string(),
 161:             ],
 162:         ) {
 163:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
 164:             return Err(err);
 165:         }
 166:         if let Err(err) = self.runner.run(
 167:             "ip",
 168:             &[
 169:                 "address".to_string(),
 170:                 "add".to_string(),
 171:                 context.local_cidr.clone(),
 172:                 "dev".to_string(),
 173:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 174:             ],
 175:         ) {
 176:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
 177:             return Err(err);
 178:         }
 179:         if let Err(err) = self.runner.run(
 180:             "ip",
 181:             &[
 182:                 "link".to_string(),
 183:                 "set".to_string(),
 184:                 "up".to_string(),
 185:                 "dev".to_string(),
 186:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 187:             ],
 188:         ) {
 189:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
 190:             return Err(err);
 191:         }
 192:         Ok(())
 193:     }
 194: 
 195:     fn remove_interface(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 196:         self.runner.run(
 197:             "ip",
 198:             &[
 199:                 "link".to_string(),
 200:                 "del".to_string(),
 201:                 "dev".to_string(),
 202:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 203:             ],
 204:         )
 205:     }
 206: 
 207:     fn apply_route_reconciliation(&mut self, next_routes: &[Route]) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 208:         for route in &self.routes {
 209:             if next_routes.iter().any(|candidate| candidate == route) {
 210:                 continue;
 211:             }
 212: 
 213:             match self.runner.run(
 214:                 "ip",
 215:                 &[
 216:                     "route".to_string(),
 217:                     "del".to_string(),
 218:                     route.destination_cidr.clone(),
 219:                     "dev".to_string(),
 220:                     self.interface_name.clone(),
 221:                 ],
 222:             ) {
 223:                 Ok(()) => {}
 224:                 Err(err) => {
 225:                     if !Self::is_missing_ip_route_error(&err) {
 226:                         return Err(err);
 227:                     }
 228:                 }
 229:             }
 230:         }
 231: 
 232:         for route in next_routes {
 233:             Self::ensure_cidr(&route.destination_cidr)?;
 234:             if matches!(route.kind, rustynet_backend_api::RouteKind::ExitNodeDefault) {
 235:                 continue;
 236:             }
 237:             self.runner.run(
 238:                 "ip",
 239:                 &[
 240:                     "route".to_string(),
 241:                     "replace".to_string(),
 242:                     route.destination_cidr.clone(),
 243:                     "dev".to_string(),
 244:                     self.interface_name.clone(),
 245:                 ],
 246:             )?;
 247:         }
 248: 
 249:         self.routes = next_routes.to_vec();
 250:         Ok(())
 251:     }
 252: 
 253:     fn set_exit_tables(&mut self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 254:         const EXIT_RULE_PRIORITY: &str = "10000";
 255:         for _ in 0..64 {
 256:             if self
 257:                 .runner
 258:                 .run(
 259:                     "ip",
 260:                     &[
 261:                         "rule".to_string(),
 262:                         "del".to_string(),
 263:                         "table".to_string(),
 264:                         "51820".to_string(),
 265:                     ],
 266:                 )
 267:                 .is_err()
 268:             {
 269:                 break;
 270:             }
 271:         }
 272:         let delete_args = [
 273:             "rule".to_string(),
 274:             "del".to_string(),
 275:             "priority".to_string(),
 276:             EXIT_RULE_PRIORITY.to_string(),
 277:             "table".to_string(),
 278:             "51820".to_string(),
 279:         ];
 280:         match mode {
 281:             ExitMode::Off => {
 282:                 let _ = self.runner.run("ip", &delete_args);
 283:                 Ok(())
 284:             }
 285:             ExitMode::FullTunnel => {
 286:                 let _ = self.runner.run("ip", &delete_args);
 287:                 self.runner.run(
 288:                     "ip",
 289:                     &[
 290:                         "rule".to_string(),
 291:                         "add".to_string(),
 292:                         "priority".to_string(),
 293:                         EXIT_RULE_PRIORITY.to_string(),
 294:                         "table".to_string(),
 295:                         "51820".to_string(),
 296:                     ],
 297:                 )
 298:             }
 299:         }
 300:     }
 301: 
 302:     fn read_peer_latest_handshake_unix(
 303:         &mut self,
 304:         node_id: &NodeId,
 305:     ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 306:         let peer = self
 307:             .peers
 308:             .get(node_id)
 309:             .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"))?;
 310:         let output = self.runner.run_capture(
 311:             "wg",
 312:             &[
 313:                 "show".to_string(),
 314:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 315:                 "latest-handshakes".to_string(),
 316:             ],
 317:         )?;
 318:         let public_key = encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key);
 319:         parse_peer_latest_handshake_unix(&output.stdout, &public_key, self.peers.len().max(1))
 320:     }
 321: }
 322: 
 323: impl<R: WireguardCommandRunner + Send + Sync> TunnelBackend for LinuxWireguardBackend<R> {
 324:     fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
 325:         "wireguard-linux"
 326:     }
 327: 
 328:     fn capabilities(&self) -> BackendCapabilities {
 329:         BackendCapabilities {
 330:             supports_roaming: true,
 331:             supports_exit_nodes: true,
 332:             supports_lan_routes: true,
 333:             supports_ipv6: true,
 334:         }
 335:     }
 336: 
 337:     fn start(&mut self, context: RuntimeContext) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 338:         if self.running {
 339:             return Err(BackendError::already_running(
 340:                 "linux wireguard backend already started",
 341:             ));
 342:         }
 343:         self.configure_interface(&context)?;
 344:         self.context = Some(context);
 345:         self.running = true;
 346:         Ok(())
 347:     }
 348: 
 349:     fn configure_peer(&mut self, peer: PeerConfig) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 350:         self.ensure_running()?;
 351:         if peer.allowed_ips.is_empty() {
 352:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 353:                 "peer allowed_ips must not be empty",
 354:             ));
 355:         }
 356: 
 357:         for cidr in &peer.allowed_ips {
 358:             Self::ensure_cidr(cidr)?;
 359:         }
 360: 
 361:         let allowed_ips = peer.allowed_ips.join(",");
 362:         let endpoint = format!("{}:{}", peer.endpoint.addr, peer.endpoint.port);
 363:         self.runner.run(
 364:             "wg",
 365:             &[
 366:                 "set".to_string(),
 367:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 368:                 "peer".to_string(),
 369:                 encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key),
 370:                 "endpoint".to_string(),
 371:                 endpoint,
 372:                 "allowed-ips".to_string(),
 373:                 allowed_ips,
 374:             ],
 375:         )?;
 376: 
 377:         self.peers.insert(peer.node_id.clone(), peer);
 378:         Ok(())
 379:     }
 380: 
 381:     fn update_peer_endpoint(
 382:         &mut self,
 383:         node_id: &NodeId,
 384:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 385:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 386:         self.ensure_running()?;
 387:         let Some(peer) = self.peers.get_mut(node_id) else {
 388:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 389:         };
 390:         let endpoint_value = format!("{}:{}", endpoint.addr, endpoint.port);
 391:         self.runner.run(
 392:             "wg",
 393:             &[
 394:                 "set".to_string(),
 395:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 396:                 "peer".to_string(),
 397:                 encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key),
 398:                 "endpoint".to_string(),
 399:                 endpoint_value,
 400:             ],
 401:         )?;
 402:         peer.endpoint = endpoint;
 403:         Ok(())
 404:     }
 405: 
 406:     fn current_peer_endpoint(
 407:         &self,
 408:         node_id: &NodeId,
 409:     ) -> Result<Option<SocketEndpoint>, BackendError> {
 410:         self.ensure_running()?;
 411:         Ok(self.peers.get(node_id).map(|peer| peer.endpoint))
 412:     }
 413: 
 414:     fn peer_latest_handshake_unix(
 415:         &mut self,
 416:         node_id: &NodeId,
 417:     ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 418:         self.ensure_running()?;
 419:         self.read_peer_latest_handshake_unix(node_id)
 420:     }
 421: 
 422:     fn remove_peer(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 423:         self.ensure_running()?;
 424:         let Some(peer) = self.peers.remove(node_id) else {
 425:             return Ok(());
 426:         };
 427:         self.runner.run(
 428:             "wg",
 429:             &[
 430:                 "set".to_string(),
 431:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 432:                 "peer".to_string(),
 433:                 encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key),
 434:                 "remove".to_string(),
 435:             ],
 436:         )
 437:     }
 438: 
 439:     fn apply_routes(&mut self, routes: Vec<Route>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 440:         self.ensure_running()?;
 441:         self.apply_route_reconciliation(&routes)
 442:     }
 443: 
 444:     fn set_exit_mode(&mut self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 445:         self.ensure_running()?;
 446:         self.set_exit_tables(mode)?;
 447:         self.exit_mode = mode;
 448:         Ok(())
 449:     }
 450: 
 451:     fn stats(&self) -> Result<TunnelStats, BackendError> {
 452:         self.ensure_running()?;
 453:         Ok(TunnelStats {
 454:             peer_count: self.peers.len(),
 455:             bytes_tx: 0,
 456:             bytes_rx: 0,
 457:             using_relay_path: false,
 458:         })
 459:     }
 460: 
 461:     fn transport_socket_identity_blocker(&self) -> Option<String> {
 462:         Some(
 463:             "linux wireguard backend is a command-only adapter over an OS-managed WireGuard UDP socket; it exposes configuration and handshake queries but no authoritative packet-I/O handle or backend-owned datagram multiplexer, so the daemon cannot safely run STUN or relay bootstrap/refresh on the real peer-traffic transport, and a same-port daemon side socket is not authoritative transport identity".to_string(),
 464:         )
 465:     }
 466: 
 467:     fn shutdown(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 468:         self.ensure_running()?;
 469:         let _ = self.set_exit_tables(ExitMode::Off);
 470:         self.remove_interface()?;
 471:         self.running = false;
 472:         self.peers.clear();
 473:         self.routes.clear();
 474:         self.context = None;
 475:         self.exit_mode = ExitMode::Off;
 476:         Ok(())
 477:     }
 478: }
 479: 
 480: pub(crate) fn validate_interface_name(name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 481:     if name.is_empty() || name.len() > 15 {
 482:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 483:             "wireguard interface name length must be between 1 and 15",
 484:         ));
 485:     }
 486:     if !name
 487:         .chars()
 488:         .all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || ch == '-' || ch == '_')
 489:     {
 490:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 491:             "wireguard interface name contains invalid characters",
 492:         ));
 493:     }
 494:     Ok(())
 495: }
 496: 
 497: pub(crate) fn validate_private_key_path(path: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 498:     if path.trim().is_empty() {
 499:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 500:             "wireguard private key path must not be empty",
 501:         ));
 502:     }
 503:     if !path.starts_with('/') {
 504:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 505:             "wireguard private key path must be absolute",
 506:         ));
 507:     }
 508:     if path.contains('\0') {
 509:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 510:             "wireguard private key path contains invalid characters",
 511:         ));
 512:     }
 513:     Ok(())
 514: }
 515: 
 516: pub(crate) fn validate_listen_port(port: u16) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 517:     if port == 0 {
 518:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 519:             "wireguard listen port must be in range 1-65535",
 520:         ));
 521:     }
 522:     Ok(())
 523: }
 524: 
 525: pub(crate) fn parse_peer_latest_handshake_unix(
 526:     stdout: &str,
 527:     expected_public_key: &str,
 528:     max_lines: usize,
 529: ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 530:     if stdout.len() > WG_LATEST_HANDSHAKES_MAX_BYTES {
 531:         return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 532:             "wg latest-handshakes output exceeded {WG_LATEST_HANDSHAKES_MAX_BYTES} bytes"
 533:         )));
 534:     }
 535: 
 536:     let mut lines_seen = 0usize;
 537:     let mut matched: Option<Option<u64>> = None;
 538:     for raw_line in stdout.lines() {
 539:         let line = raw_line.trim();
 540:         if line.is_empty() {
 541:             continue;
 542:         }
 543:         lines_seen = lines_seen.saturating_add(1);
 544:         if lines_seen > max_lines {
 545:             return Err(BackendError::internal(
 546:                 "wg latest-handshakes output exceeded expected peer count",
 547:             ));
 548:         }
 549:         let mut fields = line.split_whitespace();
 550:         let public_key = fields.next().ok_or_else(|| {
 551:             BackendError::internal("wg latest-handshakes line missing public key")
 552:         })?;
 553:         let handshake_raw = fields.next().ok_or_else(|| {
 554:             BackendError::internal("wg latest-handshakes line missing handshake timestamp")
 555:         })?;
 556:         if fields.next().is_some() {
 557:             return Err(BackendError::internal(
 558:                 "wg latest-handshakes line contains unexpected trailing data",
 559:             ));
 560:         }
 561:         if public_key != expected_public_key {
 562:             continue;
 563:         }
 564:         if matched.is_some() {
 565:             return Err(BackendError::internal(
 566:                 "wg latest-handshakes output contained duplicate peer entries",
 567:             ));
 568:         }
 569:         let handshake_unix = handshake_raw.parse::<u64>().map_err(|err| {
 570:             BackendError::internal(format!(
 571:                 "wg latest-handshakes timestamp parse failed: {err}"
 572:             ))
 573:         })?;
 574:         matched = Some((handshake_unix != 0).then_some(handshake_unix));
 575:     }
 576: 
 577:     Ok(matched.flatten())
 578: }
 579: 
 580: pub(crate) fn encode_wg_public_key_base64(value: &[u8; 32]) -> String {
 581:     const BASE64_TABLE: &[u8; 64] =
 582:         b"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
 583:     let mut output = String::with_capacity(44);
 584:     let mut index = 0usize;
 585:     while index + 3 <= value.len() {
 586:         let chunk = ((value[index] as u32) << 16)
 587:             | ((value[index + 1] as u32) << 8)
 588:             | (value[index + 2] as u32);
 589:         output.push(BASE64_TABLE[((chunk >> 18) & 0x3f) as usize] as char);
 590:         output.push(BASE64_TABLE[((chunk >> 12) & 0x3f) as usize] as char);
 591:         output.push(BASE64_TABLE[((chunk >> 6) & 0x3f) as usize] as char);
 592:         output.push(BASE64_TABLE[(chunk & 0x3f) as usize] as char);
 593:         index += 3;
 594:     }
 595: 
 596:     let remaining = value.len() - index;
 597:     if remaining == 2 {
 598:         let chunk = ((value[index] as u32) << 16) | ((value[index + 1] as u32) << 8);
 599:         output.push(BASE64_TABLE[((chunk >> 18) & 0x3f) as usize] as char);
 600:         output.push(BASE64_TABLE[((chunk >> 12) & 0x3f) as usize] as char);
 601:         output.push(BASE64_TABLE[((chunk >> 6) & 0x3f) as usize] as char);
 602:         output.push('=');
 603:     }
 604: 
 605:     output
 606: }

 608: #[cfg(test)]
 609: mod tests {
 610:     use super::*;
 611: 
 612:     #[derive(Debug, Default)]
 613:     struct RecordingRunner {
 614:         calls: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>,
 615:         fail_program: Option<String>,
 616:         capture_outputs: BTreeMap<(String, Vec<String>), WireguardCommandOutput>,
 617:     }
 618: 
 619:     impl RecordingRunner {
 620:         fn fail_on(mut self, program: &str) -> Self {
 621:             self.fail_program = Some(program.to_string());
 622:             self
 623:         }
 624: 
 625:         fn capture_output(
 626:             mut self,
 627:             program: &str,
 628:             args: &[String],
 629:             stdout: &str,
 630:             stderr: &str,
 631:         ) -> Self {
 632:             self.capture_outputs.insert(
 633:                 (program.to_string(), args.to_vec()),
 634:                 WireguardCommandOutput {
 635:                     stdout: stdout.to_string(),
 636:                     stderr: stderr.to_string(),
 637:                 },
 638:             );
 639:             self
 640:         }
 641:     }
 642: 
 643:     impl WireguardCommandRunner for RecordingRunner {
 644:         fn run(&mut self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 645:             self.calls.push((program.to_string(), args.to_vec()));
 646:             if self
 647:                 .fail_program
 648:                 .as_ref()
 649:                 .map(|candidate| candidate == program)
 650:                 .unwrap_or(false)
 651:             {
 652:                 return Err(BackendError::internal("injected failure"));
 653:             }
 654:             Ok(())
 655:         }
 656: 
 657:         fn run_capture(
 658:             &mut self,
 659:             program: &str,
 660:             args: &[String],
 661:         ) -> Result<WireguardCommandOutput, BackendError> {
 662:             self.run(program, args)?;
 663:             Ok(self
 664:                 .capture_outputs
 665:                 .get(&(program.to_string(), args.to_vec()))
 666:                 .cloned()
 667:                 .unwrap_or(WireguardCommandOutput {
 668:                     stdout: String::new(),
 669:                     stderr: String::new(),
 670:                 }))
 671:         }
 672:     }
 673: 
 674:     #[derive(Debug, Default)]
 675:     struct MissingRouteDeleteRunner;
 676: 
 677:     impl WireguardCommandRunner for MissingRouteDeleteRunner {
 678:         fn run(&mut self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 679:             let is_route_delete = program == "ip"
 680:                 && args.first().map(String::as_str) == Some("route")
 681:                 && args.get(1).map(String::as_str) == Some("del");
 682:             if is_route_delete {
 683:                 return Err(BackendError::internal(
 684:                     "privileged helper ip exited with status 2: RTNETLINK answers: No such process",
 685:                 ));
 686:             }
 687:             Ok(())
 688:         }
 689: 
 690:         fn run_capture(
 691:             &mut self,
 692:             program: &str,
 693:             args: &[String],
 694:         ) -> Result<WireguardCommandOutput, BackendError> {
 695:             self.run(program, args)?;
 696:             Ok(WireguardCommandOutput {
 697:                 stdout: String::new(),
 698:                 stderr: String::new(),
 699:             })
 700:         }
 701:     }

 703:     fn runtime_context() -> RuntimeContext {
 704:         RuntimeContext {
 705:             local_node: NodeId::new("local-node").expect("valid node id"),
 706:             interface_name: "rustynet0".to_string(),
 707:             mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.1/32".to_string(),
 708:             local_cidr: "100.64.0.1/32".to_string(),
 709:         }
 710:     }

 724:     #[test]
 725:     fn linux_backend_reports_transport_socket_identity_blocker() {
 726:         let backend = LinuxWireguardBackend::new(
 727:             RecordingRunner::default(),
 728:             "rustynet0",
 729:             "/tmp/rustynet-test.key",
 730:             51820,
 731:         )
 732:         .expect("linux backend should be constructible");
 733: 
 734:         let blocker = backend
 735:             .transport_socket_identity_blocker()
 736:             .expect("linux backend should report transport blocker");
 737:         assert!(blocker.contains("command-only adapter"));
 738:         assert!(blocker.contains("OS-managed WireGuard UDP socket"));
 739:         assert!(blocker.contains("no authoritative packet-I/O handle"));
 740:         assert!(blocker.contains("backend-owned datagram multiplexer"));
 741:         assert!(blocker.contains("same-port daemon side socket is not authoritative"));
 742:     }

===== /crates/rustynet-backend-wireguard/src/macos_command.rs =====

   1: use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
   2: use std::io::ErrorKind;
   3: use std::process::Command;
   4: 
   5: use rustynet_backend_api::{
   6:     BackendCapabilities, BackendError, ExitMode, NodeId, PeerConfig, Route, RuntimeContext,
   7:     SocketEndpoint, TunnelBackend, TunnelStats,
   8: };
   9: 
  10: use crate::linux_command::{
  11:     WireguardCommandRunner, encode_wg_public_key_base64, parse_peer_latest_handshake_unix,
  12:     validate_interface_name, validate_listen_port, validate_private_key_path,
  13: };
  14: 
  15: const MACOS_ROUTE_BINARY: &str = "/sbin/route";
  16: const MACOS_PS_BINARY: &str = "/bin/ps";
  17: 
  18: #[derive(Debug)]
  19: pub struct MacosWireguardBackend<R: WireguardCommandRunner> {
  20:     runner: R,
  21:     interface_name: String,
  22:     private_key_path: String,
  23:     egress_interface: String,
  24:     listen_port: u16,
  25:     running: bool,
  26:     peers: BTreeMap<NodeId, PeerConfig>,
  27:     routes: Vec<Route>,
  28:     context: Option<RuntimeContext>,
  29:     exit_mode: ExitMode,
  30:     default_gateway: Option<String>,
  31:     endpoint_bypass_hosts: BTreeSet<String>,
  32: }
  33: 
  34: impl<R: WireguardCommandRunner> MacosWireguardBackend<R> {
  35:     pub fn new(
  36:         runner: R,
  37:         interface_name: impl Into<String>,
  38:         private_key_path: impl Into<String>,
  39:         egress_interface: impl Into<String>,
  40:         listen_port: u16,
  41:     ) -> Result<Self, BackendError> {
  42:         let interface_name = interface_name.into();
  43:         let private_key_path = private_key_path.into();
  44:         let egress_interface = egress_interface.into();
  45:         validate_macos_interface_name(&interface_name)?;
  46:         validate_private_key_path(&private_key_path)?;
  47:         validate_interface_name(&egress_interface)?;
  48:         validate_listen_port(listen_port)?;
  49:         Ok(Self {
  50:             runner,
  51:             interface_name,
  52:             private_key_path,
  53:             egress_interface,
  54:             listen_port,
  55:             running: false,
  56:             peers: BTreeMap::new(),
  57:             routes: Vec::new(),
  58:             context: None,
  59:             exit_mode: ExitMode::Off,
  60:             default_gateway: None,
  61:             endpoint_bypass_hosts: BTreeSet::new(),
  62:         })
  63:     }
  64: 
  65:     fn ensure_running(&self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  66:         if self.running {
  67:             return Ok(());
  68:         }
  69:         Err(BackendError::not_running(
  70:             "macos wireguard backend is not running",
  71:         ))
  72:     }
  73: 
  74:     fn ensure_cidr(value: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  75:         if value.is_empty() || !value.contains('/') {
  76:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr value"));
  77:         }
  78:         if !value
  79:             .chars()
  80:             .all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || ch == '.' || ch == ':' || ch == '/')
  81:         {
  82:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
  83:                 "cidr contains invalid characters",
  84:             ));
  85:         }
  86:         Ok(())
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     fn configure_interface(&mut self, context: &RuntimeContext) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  90:         Self::ensure_cidr(&context.local_cidr)?;
  91:         let local_ip = extract_ip_from_cidr(&context.local_cidr)?;
  92: 
  93:         if self
  94:             .runner
  95:             .run("wireguard-go", std::slice::from_ref(&self.interface_name))
  96:             .is_err()
  97:         {
  98:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
  99:             self.runner
 100:                 .run("wireguard-go", std::slice::from_ref(&self.interface_name))?;
 101:         }
 102: 
 103:         if let Err(err) = self.runner.run(
 104:             "wg",
 105:             &[
 106:                 "set".to_string(),
 107:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 108:                 "private-key".to_string(),
 109:                 self.private_key_path.clone(),
 110:                 "listen-port".to_string(),
 111:                 self.listen_port.to_string(),
 112:             ],
 113:         ) {
 114:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
 115:             return Err(err);
 116:         }
 117: 
 118:         if let Err(err) = self.runner.run(
 119:             "ifconfig",
 120:             &[
 121:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 122:                 "inet".to_string(),
 123:                 local_ip.clone(),
 124:                 local_ip,
 125:                 "netmask".to_string(),
 126:                 "255.255.255.255".to_string(),
 127:             ],
 128:         ) {
 129:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
 130:             return Err(err);
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         if let Err(err) = self
 134:             .runner
 135:             .run("ifconfig", &[self.interface_name.clone(), "up".to_string()])
 136:         {
 137:             let _ = self.remove_interface();
 138:             return Err(err);
 139:         }
 140: 
 141:         Ok(())
 142:     }
 143: 
 144:     fn remove_interface(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 145:         let _ = self.runner.run(
 146:             "ifconfig",
 147:             &[self.interface_name.clone(), "down".to_string()],
 148:         );
 149:         self.restore_default_route();
 150:         self.endpoint_bypass_hosts.clear();
 151:         self.terminate_wireguard_go_processes()
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     fn apply_route_reconciliation(&mut self, next_routes: &[Route]) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 155:         for route in &self.routes {
 156:             if route.destination_cidr == "0.0.0.0/0" || route.destination_cidr == "::/0" {
 157:                 continue;
 158:             }
 159:             if !next_routes.iter().any(|candidate| candidate == route) {
 160:                 let args = route_delete_args(&route.destination_cidr)?;
 161:                 self.runner.run("route", &args)?;
 162:             }
 163:         }
 164: 
 165:         for route in next_routes {
 166:             if route.destination_cidr == "0.0.0.0/0" || route.destination_cidr == "::/0" {
 167:                 continue;
 168:             }
 169:             Self::ensure_cidr(&route.destination_cidr)?;
 170:             let args = route_add_args(&route.destination_cidr, &self.interface_name)?;
 171:             self.runner.run("route", &args)?;
 172:         }
 173: 
 174:         self.routes = next_routes.to_vec();
 175:         Ok(())
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     fn capture_default_gateway(&self) -> Result<String, BackendError> {
 179:         let output = Command::new(MACOS_ROUTE_BINARY)
 180:             .args(["-n", "get", "default"])
 181:             .output()
 182:             .map_err(|err| BackendError::internal(format!("route get default failed: {err}")))?;
 183:         if !output.status.success() {
 184:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 185:                 "route get default exited with status {}",
 186:                 output.status
 187:             )));
 188:         }
 189:         let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
 190:         for line in stdout.lines() {
 191:             let normalized = line.trim();
 192:             if let Some(value) = normalized.strip_prefix("gateway:") {
 193:                 let gateway = value.trim();
 194:                 if !gateway.is_empty() {
 195:                     return Ok(gateway.to_string());
 196:                 }
 197:             }
 198:         }
 199:         Err(BackendError::internal(
 200:             "default gateway not found in route output",
 201:         ))
 202:     }
 203: 
 204:     fn install_endpoint_bypass_routes(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 205:         let gateway = self
 206:             .default_gateway
 207:             .as_ref()
 208:             .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::internal("default gateway not captured"))?
 209:             .clone();
 210: 
 211:         self.endpoint_bypass_hosts.clear();
 212:         for peer in self.peers.values() {
 213:             let endpoint = peer.endpoint.addr.to_string();
 214:             let family = if peer.endpoint.addr.is_ipv4() {
 215:                 "-inet"
 216:             } else {
 217:                 "-inet6"
 218:             };
 219:             self.runner.run(
 220:                 "route",
 221:                 &[
 222:                     "-n".to_string(),
 223:                     "add".to_string(),
 224:                     family.to_string(),
 225:                     "-host".to_string(),
 226:                     endpoint.clone(),
 227:                     gateway.clone(),
 228:                     "-ifscope".to_string(),
 229:                     self.egress_interface.clone(),
 230:                 ],
 231:             )?;
 232:             self.endpoint_bypass_hosts.insert(endpoint);
 233:         }
 234:         Ok(())
 235:     }
 236: 
 237:     fn remove_endpoint_bypass_routes(&mut self) {
 238:         for endpoint in self.endpoint_bypass_hosts.iter().cloned() {
 239:             let is_ipv6 = endpoint.contains(':');
 240:             let family = if is_ipv6 { "-inet6" } else { "-inet" };
 241:             let _ = self.runner.run(
 242:                 "route",
 243:                 &[
 244:                     "-n".to_string(),
 245:                     "delete".to_string(),
 246:                     family.to_string(),
 247:                     "-host".to_string(),
 248:                     endpoint,
 249:                 ],
 250:             );
 251:         }
 252:         self.endpoint_bypass_hosts.clear();
 253:     }
 254: 
 255:     fn apply_default_route_to_tunnel(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 256:         let gateway = self.capture_default_gateway()?;
 257:         self.default_gateway = Some(gateway);
 258:         self.install_endpoint_bypass_routes()?;
 259: 
 260:         if self
 261:             .runner
 262:             .run(
 263:                 "route",
 264:                 &[
 265:                     "-n".to_string(),
 266:                     "change".to_string(),
 267:                     "-inet".to_string(),
 268:                     "default".to_string(),
 269:                     "-interface".to_string(),
 270:                     self.interface_name.clone(),
 271:                 ],
 272:             )
 273:             .is_err()
 274:         {
 275:             self.runner.run(
 276:                 "route",
 277:                 &[
 278:                     "-n".to_string(),
 279:                     "add".to_string(),
 280:                     "-inet".to_string(),
 281:                     "default".to_string(),
 282:                     "-interface".to_string(),
 283:                     self.interface_name.clone(),
 284:                 ],
 285:             )?;
 286:         }
 287:         Ok(())
 288:     }
 289: 
 290:     fn restore_default_route(&mut self) {
 291:         let Some(gateway) = self.default_gateway.clone() else {
 292:             return;
 293:         };
 294:         let _ = self.runner.run(
 295:             "route",
 296:             &[
 297:                 "-n".to_string(),
 298:                 "change".to_string(),
 299:                 "-inet".to_string(),
 300:                 "default".to_string(),
 301:                 gateway.clone(),
 302:             ],
 303:         );
 304:         self.remove_endpoint_bypass_routes();
 305:         self.default_gateway = None;
 306:     }
 307: 
 308:     fn terminate_wireguard_go_processes(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 309:         let mut last_error: Option<BackendError> = None;
 310:         for pid in find_wireguard_go_pids(&self.interface_name)? {
 311:             if let Err(err) = self
 312:                 .runner
 313:                 .run("kill", &["-TERM".to_string(), pid.to_string()])
 314:             {
 315:                 last_error = Some(err);
 316:             }
 317:         }
 318:         if let Some(err) = last_error {
 319:             return Err(err);
 320:         }
 321:         Ok(())
 322:     }
 323: 
 324:     fn set_exit_tables(&mut self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 325:         match mode {
 326:             ExitMode::Off => {
 327:                 self.restore_default_route();
 328:                 Ok(())
 329:             }
 330:             ExitMode::FullTunnel => self.apply_default_route_to_tunnel(),
 331:         }
 332:     }
 333: 
 334:     fn read_peer_latest_handshake_unix(
 335:         &mut self,
 336:         node_id: &NodeId,
 337:     ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 338:         let peer = self
 339:             .peers
 340:             .get(node_id)
 341:             .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"))?;
 342:         let output = self.runner.run_capture(
 343:             "wg",
 344:             &[
 345:                 "show".to_string(),
 346:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 347:                 "latest-handshakes".to_string(),
 348:             ],
 349:         )?;
 350:         let public_key = encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key);
 351:         parse_peer_latest_handshake_unix(&output.stdout, &public_key, self.peers.len().max(1))
 352:     }
 353: }
 354: 
 355: impl<R: WireguardCommandRunner + Send + Sync> TunnelBackend for MacosWireguardBackend<R> {
 356:     fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
 357:         "wireguard-macos"
 358:     }
 359: 
 360:     fn capabilities(&self) -> BackendCapabilities {
 361:         BackendCapabilities {
 362:             supports_roaming: true,
 363:             supports_exit_nodes: true,
 364:             supports_lan_routes: true,
 365:             supports_ipv6: false,
 366:         }
 367:     }
 368: 
 369:     fn start(&mut self, context: RuntimeContext) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 370:         if self.running {
 371:             return Err(BackendError::already_running(
 372:                 "macos wireguard backend already started",
 373:             ));
 374:         }
 375:         self.configure_interface(&context)?;
 376:         self.context = Some(context);
 377:         self.running = true;
 378:         Ok(())
 379:     }
 380: 
 381:     fn configure_peer(&mut self, peer: PeerConfig) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 382:         self.ensure_running()?;
 383:         if peer.allowed_ips.is_empty() {
 384:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 385:                 "peer allowed_ips must not be empty",
 386:             ));
 387:         }
 388: 
 389:         for cidr in &peer.allowed_ips {
 390:             Self::ensure_cidr(cidr)?;
 391:         }
 392: 
 393:         let allowed_ips = peer.allowed_ips.join(",");
 394:         let endpoint = format!("{}:{}", peer.endpoint.addr, peer.endpoint.port);
 395:         self.runner.run(
 396:             "wg",
 397:             &[
 398:                 "set".to_string(),
 399:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 400:                 "peer".to_string(),
 401:                 encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key),
 402:                 "endpoint".to_string(),
 403:                 endpoint,
 404:                 "allowed-ips".to_string(),
 405:                 allowed_ips,
 406:             ],
 407:         )?;
 408: 
 409:         self.peers.insert(peer.node_id.clone(), peer);
 410:         Ok(())
 411:     }
 412: 
 413:     fn update_peer_endpoint(
 414:         &mut self,
 415:         node_id: &NodeId,
 416:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 417:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 418:         self.ensure_running()?;
 419:         let Some(peer) = self.peers.get_mut(node_id) else {
 420:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 421:         };
 422:         let endpoint_value = format!("{}:{}", endpoint.addr, endpoint.port);
 423:         self.runner.run(
 424:             "wg",
 425:             &[
 426:                 "set".to_string(),
 427:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 428:                 "peer".to_string(),
 429:                 encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key),
 430:                 "endpoint".to_string(),
 431:                 endpoint_value,
 432:             ],
 433:         )?;
 434:         peer.endpoint = endpoint;
 435:         Ok(())
 436:     }
 437: 
 438:     fn current_peer_endpoint(
 439:         &self,
 440:         node_id: &NodeId,
 441:     ) -> Result<Option<SocketEndpoint>, BackendError> {
 442:         self.ensure_running()?;
 443:         Ok(self.peers.get(node_id).map(|peer| peer.endpoint))
 444:     }
 445: 
 446:     fn peer_latest_handshake_unix(
 447:         &mut self,
 448:         node_id: &NodeId,
 449:     ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 450:         self.ensure_running()?;
 451:         self.read_peer_latest_handshake_unix(node_id)
 452:     }
 453: 
 454:     fn remove_peer(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 455:         self.ensure_running()?;
 456:         let Some(peer) = self.peers.remove(node_id) else {
 457:             return Ok(());
 458:         };
 459:         self.runner.run(
 460:             "wg",
 461:             &[
 462:                 "set".to_string(),
 463:                 self.interface_name.clone(),
 464:                 "peer".to_string(),
 465:                 encode_wg_public_key_base64(&peer.public_key),
 466:                 "remove".to_string(),
 467:             ],
 468:         )
 469:     }
 470: 
 471:     fn apply_routes(&mut self, routes: Vec<Route>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 472:         self.ensure_running()?;
 473:         self.apply_route_reconciliation(&routes)
 474:     }
 475: 
 476:     fn set_exit_mode(&mut self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 477:         self.ensure_running()?;
 478:         self.set_exit_tables(mode)?;
 479:         self.exit_mode = mode;
 480:         Ok(())
 481:     }
 482: 
 483:     fn stats(&self) -> Result<TunnelStats, BackendError> {
 484:         self.ensure_running()?;
 485:         Ok(TunnelStats {
 486:             peer_count: self.peers.len(),
 487:             bytes_tx: 0,
 488:             bytes_rx: 0,
 489:             using_relay_path: false,
 490:         })
 491:     }
 492: 
 493:     fn transport_socket_identity_blocker(&self) -> Option<String> {
 494:         Some(
 495:             "macos wireguard backend is a command-only adapter over wireguard-go and its OS-managed UDP socket; it exposes configuration and handshake queries but no authoritative packet-I/O handle or backend-owned datagram multiplexer, so the daemon cannot safely run STUN or relay bootstrap/refresh on the real peer-traffic transport, and a same-port daemon side socket is not authoritative transport identity".to_string(),
 496:         )
 497:     }
 498: 
 499:     fn shutdown(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 500:         self.ensure_running()?;
 501:         let _ = self.set_exit_tables(ExitMode::Off);
 502:         self.remove_interface()?;
 503:         self.running = false;
 504:         self.peers.clear();
 505:         self.routes.clear();
 506:         self.context = None;
 507:         self.exit_mode = ExitMode::Off;
 508:         Ok(())
 509:     }
 510: }
 511: 
 512: fn validate_macos_interface_name(name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 513:     validate_interface_name(name)?;
 514:     if !name.starts_with("utun") {
 515:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 516:             "macos wireguard interface name must start with utun",
 517:         ));
 518:     }
 519:     let suffix = &name[4..];
 520:     if suffix.is_empty() || !suffix.chars().all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_digit()) {
 521:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 522:             "macos wireguard interface name must be utun followed by digits",
 523:         ));
 524:     }
 525:     Ok(())
 526: }
 527: 
 528: fn extract_ip_from_cidr(cidr: &str) -> Result<String, BackendError> {
 529:     let (ip, prefix) = cidr
 530:         .split_once('/')
 531:         .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr value"))?;
 532:     if ip.is_empty() || prefix.is_empty() {
 533:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr value"));
 534:     }
 535:     if !ip
 536:         .chars()
 537:         .all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || ch == '.' || ch == ':')
 538:     {
 539:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 540:             "cidr contains invalid characters",
 541:         ));
 542:     }
 543:     if !prefix.chars().all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_digit()) {
 544:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr prefix"));
 545:     }
 546:     Ok(ip.to_string())
 547: }
 548: 
 549: fn route_add_args(cidr: &str, interface_name: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, BackendError> {
 550:     let (destination, prefix) = cidr
 551:         .split_once('/')
 552:         .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr value"))?;
 553:     let prefix = prefix
 554:         .parse::<u16>()
 555:         .map_err(|_| BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr prefix"))?;
 556:     if destination.contains(':') {
 557:         if prefix > 128 {
 558:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid ipv6 prefix"));
 559:         }
 560:         return Ok(vec![
 561:             "-n".to_string(),
 562:             "add".to_string(),
 563:             "-inet6".to_string(),
 564:             "-net".to_string(),
 565:             cidr.to_string(),
 566:             "-interface".to_string(),
 567:             interface_name.to_string(),
 568:         ]);
 569:     }
 570:     if prefix > 32 {
 571:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid ipv4 prefix"));
 572:     }
 573:     Ok(vec![
 574:         "-n".to_string(),
 575:         "add".to_string(),
 576:         "-inet".to_string(),
 577:         "-net".to_string(),
 578:         cidr.to_string(),
 579:         "-interface".to_string(),
 580:         interface_name.to_string(),
 581:     ])
 582: }
 583: 
 584: fn route_delete_args(cidr: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, BackendError> {
 585:     let (_destination, prefix) = cidr
 586:         .split_once('/')
 587:         .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr value"))?;
 588:     let prefix = prefix
 589:         .parse::<u16>()
 590:         .map_err(|_| BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr prefix"))?;
 591:     let is_ipv6 = cidr.contains(':');
 592:     if is_ipv6 && prefix > 128 {
 593:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid ipv6 prefix"));
 594:     }
 595:     if !is_ipv6 && prefix > 32 {
 596:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid ipv4 prefix"));
 597:     }
 598:     Ok(vec![
 599:         "-n".to_string(),
 600:         "delete".to_string(),
 601:         if is_ipv6 {
 602:             "-inet6".to_string()
 603:         } else {
 604:             "-inet".to_string()
 605:         },
 606:         "-net".to_string(),
 607:         cidr.to_string(),
 608:     ])
 609: }
 610: 
 611: fn find_wireguard_go_pids(interface_name: &str) -> Result<Vec<u32>, BackendError> {
 612:     let output = match Command::new(MACOS_PS_BINARY)
 613:         .args(["-axo", "pid=,command="])
 614:         .output()
 615:     {
 616:         Ok(output) => output,
 617:         Err(err) if err.kind() == ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => {
 618:             return Ok(Vec::new());
 619:         }
 620:         Err(err) => {
 621:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!("ps spawn failed: {err}")));
 622:         }
 623:     };
 624:     if !output.status.success() {
 625:         return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 626:             "ps exited with status {}",
 627:             output.status
 628:         )));
 629:     }
 630:     let expected_suffix = format!("wireguard-go {interface_name}");
 631:     let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
 632:     let mut pids = Vec::new();
 633:     for line in stdout.lines() {
 634:         let trimmed = line.trim();
 635:         if trimmed.is_empty() {
 636:             continue;
 637:         }
 638:         let mut parts = trimmed.split_whitespace();
 639:         let Some(pid_raw) = parts.next() else {
 640:             continue;
 641:         };
 642:         let command = trimmed
 643:             .strip_prefix(pid_raw)
 644:             .map(str::trim_start)
 645:             .unwrap_or_default();
 646:         if command.is_empty() {
 647:             continue;
 648:         }
 649:         if !command.trim_end().ends_with(expected_suffix.as_str()) {
 650:             continue;
 651:         }
 652:         let pid = pid_raw
 653:             .trim()
 654:             .parse::<u32>()
 655:             .map_err(|err| BackendError::internal(format!("invalid ps pid value: {err}")))?;
 656:         pids.push(pid);
 657:     }
 658:     Ok(pids)
 659: }

===== /crates/rustynet-backend-wireguard/src/userspace_shared/engine.rs =====

   1: use std::collections::BTreeMap;
   2: use std::fmt;
   3: use std::fs;

   6: use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

   8: use base64::prelude::*;
   9: use boringtun::noise::{Tunn, TunnResult};
  10: use boringtun::x25519::{PublicKey, StaticSecret};
  11: use rustynet_backend_api::{BackendError, NodeId, PeerConfig, SocketEndpoint};
  12: 
  13: #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
  14: const MAX_ENCRYPTED_PACKET_BYTES: usize = 65_535 + 32;
  15: const MAX_DECRYPTED_PACKET_BYTES: usize = 65_535;
  16: 
  17: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  18: pub(crate) struct RecordedPeerCiphertextIngress {
  19:     pub(crate) node_id: Option<NodeId>,
  20:     pub(crate) local_addr: SocketAddr,
  21:     pub(crate) remote_addr: SocketAddr,
  22:     pub(crate) payload: Vec<u8>,
  23:     pub(crate) transport_generation: u64,
  24: }
  25:

  27: pub(crate) struct RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket {
  28:     pub(crate) node_id: NodeId,
  29:     pub(crate) packet: Vec<u8>,
  30:     pub(crate) transport_generation: u64,
  31: }

  34: pub(crate) struct OutboundCiphertextPacket {
  35:     pub(crate) remote_addr: SocketAddr,
  36:     pub(crate) payload: Vec<u8>,
  37: }
  38: 
  39: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
  40: pub(crate) struct EngineProcessingOutcome {
  41:     pub(crate) outbound_ciphertext_packets: Vec<OutboundCiphertextPacket>,
  42:     pub(crate) tunnel_plaintext_packets: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
  43:     pub(crate) authenticated_handshake: Option<(NodeId, u64)>,
  44: }

  46: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  47: pub(crate) enum ConfigurePeerDisposition {
  48:     Added,
  49:     Replaced,
  50: }
  51: 
  52: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
