  58: pub(crate) struct UserspaceEngine {
  59:     local_static_private: StaticSecret,
  60:     local_static_public: PublicKey,
  61:     next_tunnel_index: u32,
  62:     peer_states: BTreeMap<NodeId, PeerEngineState>,
  63:     recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress: Vec<RecordedPeerCiphertextIngress>,
  64:     recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets: Vec<RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket>,
  65: }
  66: 
  67: struct PeerEngineState {
  68:     peer_static_public: PublicKey,
  69:     endpoint: SocketAddr,
  70:     allowed_ips: Vec<AllowedIpNetwork>,
  71:     tunnel: Tunn,
  72: }
  73: 
  74: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  75: struct AllowedIpNetwork {
  76:     network: IpAddr,
  77:     prefix_len: u8,
  78: }
  79: 
  80: impl UserspaceEngine {
  81:     pub(crate) fn from_private_key_file(path: &Path) -> Result<Self, BackendError> {
  82:         let encoded_private_key = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|err| {
  83:             BackendError::internal(format!(
  84:                 "linux userspace-shared private key read failed for {}: {err}",
  85:                 path.display()
  86:             ))
  87:         })?;
  88:         let trimmed_private_key = encoded_private_key.trim();
  89:         let decoded_private_key = BASE64_STANDARD
  90:             .decode(trimmed_private_key.as_bytes())
  91:             .map_err(|err| {
  92:                 BackendError::internal(format!(
  93:                     "linux userspace-shared private key decode failed for {}: {err}",
  94:                     path.display()
  95:                 ))
  96:             })?;
  97:         let private_key_bytes: [u8; 32] =
  98:             decoded_private_key.try_into().map_err(|bytes: Vec<u8>| {
  99:                 BackendError::internal(format!(
 100:                     "linux userspace-shared private key length invalid for {}: expected 32 bytes after base64 decode, got {}",
 101:                     path.display(),
 102:                     bytes.len()
 103:                 ))
 104:             })?;
 105: 
 106:         let local_static_private = StaticSecret::from(private_key_bytes);
 107:         let local_static_public = PublicKey::from(&local_static_private);
 108: 
 109:         Ok(Self {
 110:             local_static_private,
 111:             local_static_public,
 112:             next_tunnel_index: 1,
 113:             peer_states: BTreeMap::new(),
 114:             recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress: Vec::new(),
 115:             recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets: Vec::new(),
 116:         })
 117:     }
 118: 
 119:     pub(crate) fn configure_peer(
 120:         &mut self,
 121:         peer: &PeerConfig,
 122:     ) -> Result<ConfigurePeerDisposition, BackendError> {
 123:         let peer_static_public = PublicKey::from(peer.public_key);
 124:         let endpoint = socket_addr_from_endpoint(peer.endpoint);
 125:         let allowed_ips = peer
 126:             .allowed_ips
 127:             .iter()
 128:             .map(|cidr| AllowedIpNetwork::parse(cidr))
 129:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
 130:         let tunnel = Tunn::new(
 131:             self.local_static_private.clone(),
 132:             peer_static_public,
 133:             None,
 134:             None,
 135:             self.allocate_tunnel_index()?,
 136:             None,
 137:         );
 138: 
 139:         let disposition = if self.peer_states.contains_key(&peer.node_id) {
 140:             ConfigurePeerDisposition::Replaced
 141:         } else {
 142:             ConfigurePeerDisposition::Added
 143:         };
 144:         self.peer_states.insert(
 145:             peer.node_id.clone(),
 146:             PeerEngineState {
 147:                 peer_static_public,
 148:                 endpoint,
 149:                 allowed_ips,
 150:                 tunnel,
 151:             },
 152:         );
 153:         Ok(disposition)
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     pub(crate) fn update_peer_endpoint(
 157:         &mut self,
 158:         node_id: &NodeId,
 159:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 160:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 161:         let Some(peer_state) = self.peer_states.get_mut(node_id) else {
 162:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 163:         };
 164:         peer_state.endpoint = socket_addr_from_endpoint(endpoint);
 165:         Ok(())
 166:     }
 167: 
 168:     pub(crate) fn initiate_handshake(
 169:         &mut self,
 170:         node_id: &NodeId,
 171:         transport_generation: u64,
 172:         force_resend: bool,
 173:     ) -> Result<EngineProcessingOutcome, BackendError> {
 174:         let Some(peer_state) = self.peer_states.get_mut(node_id) else {
 175:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 176:         };
 177:         let mut encrypt_buf = vec![0u8; MAX_ENCRYPTED_PACKET_BYTES];
 178:         let initial_result = peer_state
 179:             .tunnel
 180:             .format_handshake_initiation(&mut encrypt_buf, force_resend);
 181:         Ok(drive_outbound_result(
 182:             node_id,
 183:             peer_state,
 184:             transport_generation,
 185:             initial_result,
 186:             &mut self.recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 187:         ))
 188:     }
 189: 
 190:     pub(crate) fn current_peer_endpoint(&self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Option<SocketEndpoint> {
 191:         self.peer_states
 192:             .get(node_id)
 193:             .map(|peer_state| SocketEndpoint {
 194:                 addr: peer_state.endpoint.ip(),
 195:                 port: peer_state.endpoint.port(),
 196:             })
 197:     }
 198: 
 199:     pub(crate) fn has_peer(&self, node_id: &NodeId) -> bool {
 200:         self.peer_states.contains_key(node_id)
 201:     }
 202: 
 203:     pub(crate) fn has_endpoint(&self, remote_addr: SocketAddr) -> bool {
 204:         self.peer_states
 205:             .values()
 206:             .any(|peer_state| peer_state.endpoint == remote_addr)
 207:     }
 208: 
 209:     pub(crate) fn remove_peer(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId) -> bool {
 210:         self.peer_states.remove(node_id).is_some()
 211:     }
 212: 
 213:     pub(crate) fn process_inbound_ciphertext(
 214:         &mut self,
 215:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 216:         local_addr: SocketAddr,
 217:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 218:         transport_generation: u64,
 219:     ) -> Result<EngineProcessingOutcome, BackendError> {
 220:         let matched_node_id = self.find_node_id_by_endpoint(remote_addr);
 221:         self.recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress
 222:             .push(RecordedPeerCiphertextIngress {
 223:                 node_id: matched_node_id.clone(),
 224:                 local_addr,
 225:                 remote_addr,
 226:                 payload: payload.clone(),
 227:                 transport_generation,
 228:             });
 229: 
 230:         let Some(node_id) = matched_node_id else {
 231:             return Ok(EngineProcessingOutcome::default());
 232:         };
 233: 
 234:         let Self {
 235:             peer_states,
 236:             recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 237:             ..
 238:         } = self;
 239:         let peer_state = peer_states
 240:             .get_mut(&node_id)
 241:             .expect("matched peer state should exist");
 242:         let mut decrypt_buf = vec![0u8; MAX_DECRYPTED_PACKET_BYTES];
 243:         let initial_result =
 244:             peer_state
 245:                 .tunnel
 246:                 .decapsulate(Some(remote_addr.ip()), &payload, &mut decrypt_buf);
 247:         Ok(drive_inbound_result(
 248:             &node_id,
 249:             peer_state,
 250:             remote_addr,
 251:             transport_generation,
 252:             initial_result,
 253:             recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 254:         ))
 255:     }
 256: 
 257:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 258:     pub(crate) fn inject_plaintext_packet(
 259:         &mut self,
 260:         packet: &[u8],
 261:         transport_generation: u64,
 262:     ) -> Result<EngineProcessingOutcome, BackendError> {
 263:         let Some(dst_addr) = Tunn::dst_address(packet) else {
 264:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 265:                 "plaintext packet does not contain a valid IPv4/IPv6 destination address",
 266:             ));
 267:         };
 268:         let node_id = self.select_peer_for_destination(dst_addr).ok_or_else(|| {
 269:             BackendError::invalid_input(
 270:                 "no configured peer allowed IP matches the plaintext packet destination",
 271:             )
 272:         })?;
 273: 
 274:         let Self {
 275:             peer_states,
 276:             recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 277:             ..
 278:         } = self;
 279:         let peer_state = peer_states
 280:             .get_mut(&node_id)
 281:             .expect("selected peer state should exist");
 282:         let mut encrypt_buf = vec![0u8; MAX_ENCRYPTED_PACKET_BYTES.max(packet.len() + 32)];
 283:         let initial_result = peer_state.tunnel.encapsulate(packet, &mut encrypt_buf);
 284:         Ok(drive_outbound_result(
 285:             &node_id,
 286:             peer_state,
 287:             transport_generation,
 288:             initial_result,
 289:             recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 290:         ))
 291:     }
 292: 
 293:     pub(crate) fn stats(&self) -> EngineStats {
 294:         let mut bytes_tx = 0u64;
 295:         let mut bytes_rx = 0u64;
 296:         for peer_state in self.peer_states.values() {
 297:             let (_handshake, peer_tx, peer_rx, _loss, _rtt) = peer_state.tunnel.stats();
 298:             bytes_tx = bytes_tx.saturating_add(peer_tx as u64);
 299:             bytes_rx = bytes_rx.saturating_add(peer_rx as u64);
 300:         }
 301:         EngineStats { bytes_tx, bytes_rx }
 302:     }
 303: 
 304:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 305:     pub(crate) fn recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress(&self) -> &[RecordedPeerCiphertextIngress] {
 306:         &self.recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress
 307:     }
 308: 
 309:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 310:     pub(crate) fn recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets(&self) -> &[RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket] {
 311:         &self.recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets
 312:     }
 313: 
 314:     fn allocate_tunnel_index(&mut self) -> Result<u32, BackendError> {
 315:         let index = self.next_tunnel_index;
 316:         self.next_tunnel_index = self.next_tunnel_index.checked_add(1).ok_or_else(|| {
 317:             BackendError::internal(
 318:                 "linux userspace-shared userspace engine exhausted peer tunnel indices",
 319:             )
 320:         })?;
 321:         Ok(index)
 322:     }
 323: 
 324:     fn find_node_id_by_endpoint(&self, remote_addr: SocketAddr) -> Option<NodeId> {
 325:         self.peer_states
 326:             .iter()
 327:             .find(|(_node_id, peer_state)| peer_state.endpoint == remote_addr)
 328:             .map(|(node_id, _peer_state)| node_id.clone())
 329:     }
 330: 
 331:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 332:     fn select_peer_for_destination(&self, dst_addr: IpAddr) -> Option<NodeId> {
 333:         self.peer_states
 334:             .iter()
 335:             .find(|(_node_id, peer_state)| {
 336:                 peer_state
 337:                     .allowed_ips
 338:                     .iter()
 339:                     .any(|allowed_ip| allowed_ip.contains(dst_addr))
 340:             })
 341:             .map(|(node_id, _peer_state)| node_id.clone())
 342:     }
 343: }
 344: 
 345: fn drive_inbound_result(
 346:     node_id: &NodeId,
 347:     peer_state: &mut PeerEngineState,
 348:     remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 349:     transport_generation: u64,
 350:     initial_result: TunnResult<'_>,
 351:     recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets: &mut Vec<RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket>,
 352: ) -> EngineProcessingOutcome {
 353:     let should_drain_follow_ups = !matches!(initial_result, TunnResult::Err(_));
 354:     let mut outcome = handle_single_tunn_result(
 355:         node_id,
 356:         peer_state,
 357:         remote_addr,
 358:         transport_generation,
 359:         initial_result,
 360:         recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 361:     );
 362: 
 363:     if should_drain_follow_ups {
 364:         loop {
 365:             let mut follow_up_buf = vec![0u8; MAX_DECRYPTED_PACKET_BYTES];
 366:             let follow_up = peer_state.tunnel.decapsulate(None, &[], &mut follow_up_buf);
 367:             if matches!(follow_up, TunnResult::Done) {
 368:                 break;
 369:             }
 370:             let next = handle_single_tunn_result(
 371:                 node_id,
 372:                 peer_state,
 373:                 remote_addr,
 374:                 transport_generation,
 375:                 follow_up,
 376:                 recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 377:             );
 378:             merge_engine_processing_outcomes(&mut outcome, next);
 379:         }
 380:     }
 381: 
 382:     let observed_handshake = authenticated_handshake_unix(&peer_state.tunnel);
 383:     if let Some(observed_handshake) = observed_handshake {
 384:         outcome.authenticated_handshake = Some((node_id.clone(), observed_handshake));
 385:     }
 386:     outcome
 387: }
 388: 
 389: #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 390: fn drive_outbound_result(
 391:     node_id: &NodeId,
 392:     peer_state: &mut PeerEngineState,
 393:     transport_generation: u64,
 394:     initial_result: TunnResult<'_>,
 395:     recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets: &mut Vec<RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket>,
 396: ) -> EngineProcessingOutcome {
 397:     let mut outcome = handle_single_tunn_result(
 398:         node_id,
 399:         peer_state,
 400:         peer_state.endpoint,
 401:         transport_generation,
 402:         initial_result,
 403:         recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets,
 404:     );
 405:     let observed_handshake = authenticated_handshake_unix(&peer_state.tunnel);
 406:     if let Some(observed_handshake) = observed_handshake {
 407:         outcome.authenticated_handshake = Some((node_id.clone(), observed_handshake));
 408:     }
 409:     outcome
 410: }
 411: 
 412: fn handle_single_tunn_result(
 413:     node_id: &NodeId,
 414:     peer_state: &mut PeerEngineState,
 415:     remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 416:     transport_generation: u64,
 417:     result: TunnResult<'_>,
 418:     recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets: &mut Vec<RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket>,
 419: ) -> EngineProcessingOutcome {
 420:     let mut outcome = EngineProcessingOutcome::default();
 421:     match result {
 422:         TunnResult::Done | TunnResult::Err(_) => {}
 423:         TunnResult::WriteToNetwork(packet) => {
 424:             outcome
 425:                 .outbound_ciphertext_packets
 426:                 .push(OutboundCiphertextPacket {
 427:                     remote_addr,
 428:                     payload: packet.to_vec(),
 429:                 });
 430:         }
 431:         TunnResult::WriteToTunnelV4(packet, _src_addr) => {
 432:             let recorded_packet = RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket {
 433:                 node_id: node_id.clone(),
 434:                 packet: packet.to_vec(),
 435:                 transport_generation,
 436:             };
 437:             recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets.push(recorded_packet);
 438:             outcome.tunnel_plaintext_packets.push(packet.to_vec());
 439:         }
 440:         TunnResult::WriteToTunnelV6(packet, _src_addr) => {
 441:             let recorded_packet = RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket {
 442:                 node_id: node_id.clone(),
 443:                 packet: packet.to_vec(),
 444:                 transport_generation,
 445:             };
 446:             recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets.push(recorded_packet);
 447:             outcome.tunnel_plaintext_packets.push(packet.to_vec());
 448:         }
 449:     }
 450: 
 451:     let observed_handshake = authenticated_handshake_unix(&peer_state.tunnel);
 452:     if let Some(observed_handshake) = observed_handshake {
 453:         outcome.authenticated_handshake = Some((node_id.clone(), observed_handshake));
 454:     }
 455:     outcome
 456: }
 457: 
 458: fn authenticated_handshake_unix(tunnel: &Tunn) -> Option<u64> {
 459:     let (time_since_last_handshake, _tx, _rx, _loss, _rtt) = tunnel.stats();
 460:     let duration = time_since_last_handshake?;
 461:     let now_unix = SystemTime::now()
 462:         .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
 463:         .ok()
 464:         .map(|duration| duration.as_secs())?;
 465:     Some(now_unix.saturating_sub(duration.as_secs()))
 466: }
 467: 
 468: fn merge_engine_processing_outcomes(
 469:     current: &mut EngineProcessingOutcome,
 470:     next: EngineProcessingOutcome,
 471: ) {
 472:     current
 473:         .outbound_ciphertext_packets
 474:         .extend(next.outbound_ciphertext_packets);
 475:     current
 476:         .tunnel_plaintext_packets
 477:         .extend(next.tunnel_plaintext_packets);
 478:     match (
 479:         &current.authenticated_handshake,
 480:         next.authenticated_handshake,
 481:     ) {
 482:         (_, None) => {}
 483:         (None, Some(observed)) => current.authenticated_handshake = Some(observed),
 484:         (Some((current_node_id, current_unix)), Some((next_node_id, next_unix)))
 485:             if current_node_id == &next_node_id && next_unix > *current_unix =>
 486:         {
 487:             current.authenticated_handshake = Some((next_node_id, next_unix));
 488:         }
 489:         _ => {}
 490:     }
 491: }
 492: 
 493: fn socket_addr_from_endpoint(endpoint: SocketEndpoint) -> SocketAddr {
 494:     SocketAddr::new(endpoint.addr, endpoint.port)
 495: }
 496: 
 497: impl AllowedIpNetwork {
 498:     fn parse(value: &str) -> Result<Self, BackendError> {
 499:         let (network_str, prefix_str) = value.split_once('/').ok_or_else(|| {
 500:             BackendError::invalid_input("peer allowed_ips entries must be valid CIDR strings")
 501:         })?;
 502:         let network = network_str.parse::<IpAddr>().map_err(|err| {
 503:             BackendError::invalid_input(format!(
 504:                 "peer allowed_ips entry has invalid network address {network_str}: {err}"
 505:             ))
 506:         })?;
 507:         let prefix_len = prefix_str.parse::<u8>().map_err(|err| {
 508:             BackendError::invalid_input(format!(
 509:                 "peer allowed_ips entry has invalid prefix length {prefix_str}: {err}"
 510:             ))
 511:         })?;
 512: 
 513:         match network {
 514:             IpAddr::V4(_) if prefix_len <= 32 => Ok(Self {
 515:                 network: mask_ip(network, prefix_len),
 516:                 prefix_len,
 517:             }),
 518:             IpAddr::V6(_) if prefix_len <= 128 => Ok(Self {
 519:                 network: mask_ip(network, prefix_len),
 520:                 prefix_len,
 521:             }),
 522:             IpAddr::V4(_) => Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 523:                 "peer allowed_ips IPv4 prefix length must be <= 32",
 524:             )),
 525:             IpAddr::V6(_) => Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 526:                 "peer allowed_ips IPv6 prefix length must be <= 128",
 527:             )),
 528:         }
 529:     }
 530: 
 531:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 532:     fn contains(&self, addr: IpAddr) -> bool {
 533:         std::mem::discriminant(&self.network) == std::mem::discriminant(&addr)
 534:             && mask_ip(addr, self.prefix_len) == self.network
 535:     }
 536: }
 537: 
 538: fn mask_ip(addr: IpAddr, prefix_len: u8) -> IpAddr {
 539:     match addr {
 540:         IpAddr::V4(addr) => {
 541:             let prefix_len = prefix_len.min(32);
 542:             let mask = if prefix_len == 0 {
 543:                 0
 544:             } else {
 545:                 u32::MAX << (32 - prefix_len)
 546:             };
 547:             IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::from(u32::from(addr) & mask))
 548:         }
 549:         IpAddr::V6(addr) => {
 550:             let prefix_len = prefix_len.min(128);
 551:             let mask = if prefix_len == 0 {
 552:                 0
 553:             } else {
 554:                 u128::MAX << (128 - prefix_len)
 555:             };
 556:             IpAddr::V6(Ipv6Addr::from(u128::from(addr) & mask))
 557:         }
 558:     }
 559: }
 560: 
 561: impl fmt::Debug for UserspaceEngine {
 562:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 563:         let has_local_static_private = {
 564:             let _ = &self.local_static_private;
 565:             true
 566:         };
 567:         f.debug_struct("UserspaceEngine")
 568:             .field("has_local_static_private", &has_local_static_private)
 569:             .field("local_static_public", self.local_static_public.as_bytes())
 570:             .field("peer_count", &self.peer_states.len())
 571:             .field(
 572:                 "recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress_count",
 573:                 &self.recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress.len(),
 574:             )
 575:             .field(
 576:                 "recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets_count",
 577:                 &self.recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets.len(),
 578:             )
 579:             .finish()
 580:     }
 581: }
 582: 
 583: impl fmt::Debug for PeerEngineState {

 585:         f.debug_struct("PeerEngineState")
 586:             .field("peer_static_public", self.peer_static_public.as_bytes())
 587:             .field("endpoint", &self.endpoint)
 588:             .field("allowed_ips", &self.allowed_ips)
 589:             .finish()
 590:     }
 591: }

===== /crates/rustynet-backend-wireguard/src/userspace_shared/handshake.rs =====

   1: use std::collections::BTreeMap;
   2: 
   3: use rustynet_backend_api::NodeId;
   4: 
   5: #[derive(Debug, Default)]
   6: pub(crate) struct HandshakeTelemetry {
   7:     latest_handshakes_by_node: BTreeMap<NodeId, u64>,
   8: }
   9: 
  10: impl HandshakeTelemetry {
  11:     pub(crate) fn latest_handshake(&self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Option<u64> {
  12:         self.latest_handshakes_by_node.get(node_id).copied()
  13:     }
  14: 
  15:     pub(crate) fn record_authenticated_handshake(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId, unix_secs: u64) {
  16:         let entry = self
  17:             .latest_handshakes_by_node
  18:             .entry(node_id.clone())
  19:             .or_insert(unix_secs);
  20:         if unix_secs > *entry {
  21:             *entry = unix_secs;
  22:         }
  23:     }
  24: 
  25:     pub(crate) fn clear_peer(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId) {
  26:         self.latest_handshakes_by_node.remove(node_id);
  27:     }
  28: }

 102:         validate_interface_name(&interface_name)?;

 108:             listen_port,

 114:             desired_exit_mode: ExitMode::Off,

 117: 
 118:     fn ensure_runtime_control(&self) -> Result<&RuntimeControl, BackendError> {
 119:         self.runtime
 120:             .as_ref()
 121:             .map(RunningUserspaceRuntime::control)
 122:             .ok_or_else(|| {
 123:                 BackendError::not_running("linux userspace-shared wireguard backend is not running")
 124:             })
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     fn is_runtime_worker_unavailable(err: &BackendError) -> bool {
 128:         err.kind == rustynet_backend_api::BackendErrorKind::Internal
 129:             && matches!(
 130:                 err.message.as_str(),
 131:                 "linux userspace-shared runtime worker is unavailable"
 132:                     | "linux userspace-shared runtime worker dropped a reply"
 133:             )
 134:     }
 135: 
 136:     fn start_runtime(
 137:         &mut self,
 138:         context: RuntimeContext,
 139:     ) -> Result<RunningUserspaceRuntime, BackendError> {
 140:         let engine =
 141:             UserspaceEngine::from_private_key_file(Path::new(self.private_key_path.as_path()))?;
 142:         let tun_device = self
 143:             .tun_lifecycle
 144:             .prepare_and_open(&self.interface_name, &context)?;
 145:         let socket = match AuthoritativeSocket::bind(self.listen_port) {
 146:             Ok(socket) => socket,
 147:             Err(err) => {
 148:                 drop(tun_device);
 149:                 return Err(self.cleanup_tun_after_failed_start(err));
 150:             }
 151:         };
 152:         match RunningUserspaceRuntime::start(
 153:             &self.interface_name,
 154:             context,
 155:             tun_device,
 156:             socket,
 157:             engine,
 158:             self.tun_lifecycle.clone(),
 159:         ) {
 160:             Ok(runtime) => Ok(runtime),
 161:             Err(err) => Err(self.cleanup_tun_after_failed_start(err)),
 162:         }
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     fn recover_runtime_after_worker_exit(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 166:         let context = self.runtime_context.clone().ok_or_else(|| {
 167:             BackendError::not_running("linux userspace-shared wireguard backend is not running")
 168:         })?;
 169:         let previous_runtime = self.runtime.take().ok_or_else(|| {
 170:             BackendError::not_running("linux userspace-shared wireguard backend is not running")
 171:         })?;
 172: 
 173:         if let Err(err) = previous_runtime.shutdown()
 174:             && !Self::is_runtime_worker_unavailable(&err)
 175:         {
 176:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 177:                 "linux userspace-shared runtime recovery failed while shutting down stale runtime: {err}"
 178:             )));
 179:         }
 180: 
 181:         self.tun_lifecycle.cleanup(&self.interface_name)?;
 182:         let runtime = self.start_runtime(context.clone()).map_err(|err| {
 183:             BackendError::internal(format!(
 184:                 "linux userspace-shared runtime recovery failed while starting replacement runtime: {err}"
 185:             ))
 186:         })?;
 187:         self.runtime = Some(runtime);
 188:         self.runtime_context = Some(context);
 189: 
 190:         let replay_result = (|| -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 191:             for peer in self.desired_peers.values().cloned() {
 192:                 self.ensure_runtime_control()?.configure_peer(peer)?;
 193:             }
 194:             if !self.desired_routes.is_empty() {
 195:                 self.ensure_runtime_control()?
 196:                     .apply_routes(self.desired_routes.clone())?;
 197:             }
 198:             if self.desired_exit_mode != ExitMode::Off {
 199:                 self.ensure_runtime_control()?
 200:                     .set_exit_mode(self.desired_exit_mode)?;
 201:             }
 202:             Ok(())
 203:         })();
 204: 
 205:         if let Err(err) = replay_result {
 206:             if let Some(runtime) = self.runtime.take() {
 207:                 let _ = runtime.shutdown();
 208:             }
 209:             let _ = self.tun_lifecycle.cleanup(&self.interface_name);
 210:             return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
 211:                 "linux userspace-shared runtime recovery failed while replaying desired state: {err}"
 212:             )));
 213:         }
 214: 
 215:         Ok(())
 216:     }
 217: 
 218:     fn with_runtime_recovery<T>(
 219:         &mut self,
 220:         operation: impl Fn(&RuntimeControl) -> Result<T, BackendError>,
 221:     ) -> Result<T, BackendError> {
 222:         let first_result = {
 223:             let control = self.ensure_runtime_control()?;
 224:             operation(control)
 225:         };
 226:         match first_result {
 227:             Ok(value) => Ok(value),
 228:             Err(err) if Self::is_runtime_worker_unavailable(&err) => {
 229:                 self.recover_runtime_after_worker_exit()?;
 230:                 let control = self.ensure_runtime_control()?;
 231:                 operation(control)
 232:             }
 233:             Err(err) => Err(err),
 234:         }
 235:     }
 236: 
 237:     fn combine_cleanup_error(primary: BackendError, cleanup: BackendError) -> BackendError {
 238:         BackendError::internal(format!(
 239:             "{}; cleanup failed: {}",
 240:             primary.message, cleanup.message
 241:         ))
 242:     }
 243: 
 244:     fn cleanup_tun_after_failed_start(&mut self, err: BackendError) -> BackendError {
 245:         match self.tun_lifecycle.cleanup(&self.interface_name) {
 246:             Ok(()) => err,
 247:             Err(cleanup_err) => Self::combine_cleanup_error(err, cleanup_err),
 248:         }
 249:     }
 250: 
 251:     fn validate_peer(peer: &PeerConfig) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 252:         if peer.allowed_ips.is_empty() {
 253:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 254:                 "peer allowed_ips must not be empty",
 255:             ));
 256:         }
 257: 
 258:         for cidr in &peer.allowed_ips {
 259:             validate_cidr(cidr)?;
 260:         }
 261: 
 262:         Ok(())
 263:     }

 360: 
 361: impl TunnelBackend for LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend {
 362:     fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
 363:         LINUX_USERSPACE_SHARED_BACKEND_MODE
 364:     }
 365: 
 366:     fn capabilities(&self) -> BackendCapabilities {
 367:         BackendCapabilities {
 368:             supports_roaming: false,
 369:             supports_exit_nodes: true,
 370:             supports_lan_routes: true,
 371:             supports_ipv6: false,
 372:         }
 373:     }
 374: 
 375:     fn start(&mut self, context: RuntimeContext) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 376:         if self.runtime.is_some() {
 377:             return Err(BackendError::already_running(
 378:                 "linux userspace-shared wireguard backend already started",
 379:             ));
 380:         }
 381:         self.runtime_context = Some(context.clone());
 382:         self.desired_peers.clear();
 383:         self.desired_routes.clear();
 384:         self.desired_exit_mode = ExitMode::Off;
 385:         let runtime = match self.start_runtime(context) {
 386:             Ok(runtime) => runtime,
 387:             Err(err) => {
 388:                 self.runtime_context = None;
 389:                 return Err(err);
 390:             }
 391:         };
 392:         self.runtime = Some(runtime);
 393:         Ok(())
 394:     }
 395: 
 396:     fn configure_peer(&mut self, peer: PeerConfig) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 397:         Self::validate_peer(&peer)?;
 398:         let node_id = peer.node_id.clone();
 399:         self.with_runtime_recovery(|control| control.configure_peer(peer.clone()))?;
 400:         self.desired_peers.insert(node_id, peer);
 401:         Ok(())
 402:     }
 403: 
 404:     fn update_peer_endpoint(
 405:         &mut self,
 406:         node_id: &NodeId,
 407:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 408:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 409:         let mut updated_peer = self
 410:             .desired_peers
 411:             .get(node_id)
 412:             .cloned()
 413:             .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"))?;
 414:         self.with_runtime_recovery(|control| {
 415:             control.update_peer_endpoint(node_id.clone(), endpoint)
 416:         })?;
 417:         updated_peer.endpoint = endpoint;
 418:         self.desired_peers.insert(node_id.clone(), updated_peer);
 419:         Ok(())
 420:     }
 421: 
 422:     fn current_peer_endpoint(
 423:         &self,
 424:         node_id: &NodeId,
 425:     ) -> Result<Option<SocketEndpoint>, BackendError> {
 426:         self.ensure_runtime_control()?
 427:             .current_peer_endpoint(node_id.clone())
 428:     }
 429: 
 430:     fn peer_latest_handshake_unix(
 431:         &mut self,
 432:         node_id: &NodeId,
 433:     ) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 434:         self.with_runtime_recovery(|control| control.peer_latest_handshake_unix(node_id.clone()))
 435:     }
 436: 
 437:     fn remove_peer(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 438:         if !self.desired_peers.contains_key(node_id) {
 439:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 440:         }
 441:         self.with_runtime_recovery(|control| control.remove_peer(node_id.clone()))?;
 442:         self.desired_peers.remove(node_id);
 443:         Ok(())
 444:     }
 445: 
 446:     fn apply_routes(&mut self, routes: Vec<Route>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 447:         self.with_runtime_recovery(|control| control.apply_routes(routes.clone()))?;
 448:         self.desired_routes = routes;
 449:         Ok(())
 450:     }
 451: 
 452:     fn set_exit_mode(&mut self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 453:         self.with_runtime_recovery(|control| control.set_exit_mode(mode))?;
 454:         self.desired_exit_mode = mode;
 455:         Ok(())
 456:     }
 457: 
 458:     fn stats(&self) -> Result<TunnelStats, BackendError> {
 459:         self.ensure_runtime_control()?.stats()
 460:     }
 461: 
 462:     fn initiate_peer_handshake(
 463:         &mut self,
 464:         node_id: &NodeId,
 465:         force_resend: bool,
 466:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 467:         self.with_runtime_recovery(|control| {
 468:             control.initiate_peer_handshake(node_id.clone(), force_resend)
 469:         })
 470:     }
 471: 
 472:     fn authoritative_transport_identity(&self) -> Option<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity> {
 473:         self.runtime
 474:             .as_ref()
 475:             .map(|runtime| runtime.control().authoritative_identity())
 476:     }
 477: 
 478:     fn authoritative_transport_round_trip(
 479:         &mut self,
 480:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 481:         payload: &[u8],
 482:         timeout: Duration,
 483:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportResponse, BackendError> {
 484:         self.ensure_runtime_control()?
 485:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(remote_addr, payload.to_vec(), timeout)
 486:     }
 487: 
 488:     fn authoritative_transport_send(
 489:         &mut self,
 490:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 491:         payload: &[u8],
 492:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, BackendError> {
 493:         self.ensure_runtime_control()?
 494:             .authoritative_transport_send(remote_addr, payload.to_vec())
 495:     }
 496: 
 497:     fn shutdown(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 498:         let runtime = self.runtime.take().ok_or_else(|| {
 499:             BackendError::not_running("linux userspace-shared wireguard backend is not running")
 500:         })?;
 501:         let runtime_result = runtime.shutdown();
 502:         let cleanup_result = self.tun_lifecycle.cleanup(&self.interface_name);
 503:         self.runtime_context = None;
 504:         self.desired_peers.clear();
 505:         self.desired_routes.clear();
 506:         self.desired_exit_mode = ExitMode::Off;
 507:         match (runtime_result, cleanup_result) {
 508:             (Ok(()), Ok(())) => Ok(()),
 509:             (Err(err), Ok(())) => Err(err),
 510:             (Ok(()), Err(err)) => Err(err),
 511:             (Err(err), Err(cleanup_err)) => Err(Self::combine_cleanup_error(err, cleanup_err)),
 512:         }
 513:     }
 514: }
 515: 
 516: fn validate_cidr(value: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 517:     if value.is_empty() || !value.contains('/') {
 518:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("invalid cidr value"));
 519:     }
 520:     if !value
 521:         .chars()
 522:         .all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || ch == '.' || ch == ':' || ch == '/')
 523:     {
 524:         return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 525:             "cidr contains invalid characters",
 526:         ));
 527:     }
 528:     Ok(())
 529: }
 530: 
 531: impl RuntimeControl {
 532:     fn authoritative_transport_round_trip(
 533:         &self,
 534:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 535:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 536:         timeout: Duration,
 537:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportResponse, BackendError> {
 538:         self.authoritative_round_trip(remote_addr, payload, timeout)
 539:     }
 540: 
 541:     fn authoritative_transport_send(
 542:         &self,
 543:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 544:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 545:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, BackendError> {
 546:         self.authoritative_send(remote_addr, payload)
 547:     }
 548: }

 579:         RuntimeContext {

 581:             interface_name: "rustynet0".to_string(),
 582:             mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),

 588:         private_key_path: &Path,

 600: 
 601:     fn unique_path(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
 602:         let nanos = SystemTime::now()
 603:             .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
 604:             .expect("time should move forward")
 605:             .as_nanos();
 606:         let counter = UNIQUE_PATH_COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
 607:         std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
 608:             "rustynet-userspace-shared-{name}-{}-{nanos}-{counter}.key",
 609:             std::process::id(),
 610:         ))
 611:     }
 612: 
 613:     fn write_private_key(bytes: [u8; 32]) -> PathBuf {
 614:         let path = unique_path("valid");
 615:         fs::write(&path, format!("{}\n", BASE64_STANDARD.encode(bytes)))
 616:             .expect("private key should be written");
 617:         path
 618:     }
 619: 
 620:     fn write_invalid_private_key() -> PathBuf {
 621:         let path = unique_path("invalid");
 622:         fs::write(&path, "not-a-valid-wireguard-key\n").expect("invalid key should be written");
 623:         path
 624:     }
 625: 
 626:     fn free_listen_port() -> u16 {
 627:         let socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], 0)))
 628:             .expect("ephemeral port should be available");
 629:         socket.local_addr().expect("local addr").port()
 630:     }

 633:         SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], listen_port))
 634:     }

 646:     }

 649:         let private_key = StaticSecret::from(private_key);

 651:         *public_key.as_bytes()
 652:     }

 659:     ) -> PeerConfig {
 660:         PeerConfig {
 661:             node_id: NodeId::new(name).expect("valid node id"),

 663:                 addr: endpoint.ip(),
 664:                 port: endpoint.port(),
 665:             },
 666:             public_key,
 667:             allowed_ips: allowed_ips.into_iter().map(str::to_string).collect(),
 668:         }
 669:     }

 671:     fn route(destination_cidr: &str, kind: RouteKind) -> Route {
 672:         Route {
 673:             destination_cidr: destination_cidr.to_string(),
 674:             via_node: NodeId::new("phase1-route-node").expect("valid node id"),
 675:             kind,
 676:         }
 677:     }
 678: 
 679:     fn build_ipv4_udp_packet(src: Ipv4Addr, dst: Ipv4Addr, payload: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
 680:         let total_len = 20 + 8 + payload.len();
 681:         let udp_len = 8 + payload.len();
 682:         let mut packet = Vec::with_capacity(total_len);
 683:         packet.extend_from_slice(&[
 684:             0x45,
 685:             0x00, // version/ihl, dscp/ecn
 686:             ((total_len >> 8) & 0xff) as u8,
 687:             (total_len & 0xff) as u8,
 688:             0x00,
 689:             0x01, // identification
 690:             0x00,
 691:             0x00, // flags/fragment
 692:             64,   // ttl
 693:             17,   // udp
 694:             0x00,
 695:             0x00, // checksum ignored by boringtun
 696:         ]);
 697:         packet.extend_from_slice(&src.octets());
 698:         packet.extend_from_slice(&dst.octets());
 699:         packet.extend_from_slice(&[
 700:             0x13,
 701:             0x88, // src port 5000
 702:             0x13,
 703:             0x89, // dst port 5001
 704:             ((udp_len >> 8) & 0xff) as u8,
 705:             (udp_len & 0xff) as u8,
 706:             0x00,
 707:             0x00, // checksum ignored
 708:         ]);
 709:         packet.extend_from_slice(payload);
 710:         packet
 711:     }
 712: 
 713:     fn wait_for<T>(timeout: Duration, mut check: impl FnMut() -> Option<T>) -> T {
 714:         let start = std::time::Instant::now();
 715:         loop {
 716:             if let Some(value) = check() {
 717:                 return value;
 718:             }
 719:             if start.elapsed() >= timeout {
 720:                 panic!("condition was not satisfied within {timeout:?}");
 721:             }
 722:             thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
 723:         }
 724:     }

 727:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_identity_is_absent_before_start() {
 728:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([7; 32]);
 729:         let backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
 730:             "rustynet0",
 731:             private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
 732:             free_listen_port(),
 733:         )
 734:         .expect("backend should construct");
 735: 
 736:         assert!(backend.authoritative_transport_identity().is_none());
 737: 
 738:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
 739:     }
 740: 
 741:     #[test]
 742:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_reports_worker_owned_identity_after_start() {
 743:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([9; 32]);
 744:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
 745:         let expected_addr = SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port));
 746:         let tun_lifecycle = TestTunLifecycle::new();
 747:         let tun_state = tun_lifecycle.state();
 748:         let mut backend =
 749:             backend_with_test_tun_lifecycle(private_key_path.as_path(), listen_port, tun_lifecycle);
 750: 
 751:         backend
 752:             .start(runtime_context())
 753:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
 754: 
 755:         let identity = backend
 756:             .authoritative_transport_identity()
 757:             .expect("identity should exist after start");
 758:         assert_eq!(identity.local_addr, expected_addr);
 759:         assert_eq!(
 760:             backend
 761:                 .worker_local_addr_for_test()
 762:                 .expect("worker local addr query should succeed"),
 763:             Some(expected_addr)
 764:         );
 765:         assert_eq!(
 766:             tun_state.snapshot(),
 767:             super::tun::TunTestSnapshot {
 768:                 prepare_calls: 1,
 769:                 cleanup_calls: 0,
 770:                 live_handles: 1,
 771:                 last_interface_name: Some("rustynet0".to_string()),
 772:                 last_local_cidr: Some("100.64.0.1/32".to_string()),
 773:                 last_cleanup_interface_name: None,
 774:                 route_reconcile_calls: 0,
 775:                 last_route_interface_name: None,
 776:                 programmed_route_cidrs: Vec::new(),

 864:             .expect("listen port should be reservable for bind failure");

 893:             }

 948:     #[test]
 949:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_recovers_dead_worker_before_configure_peer() {
 950:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([13; 32]);
 951:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
 952:         let tun_lifecycle = TestTunLifecycle::new();
 953:         let tun_state = tun_lifecycle.state();
 954:         let mut backend =
 955:             backend_with_test_tun_lifecycle(private_key_path.as_path(), listen_port, tun_lifecycle);
 956:         let peer_one = peer_config(
 957:             "peer-one",
 958:             backend_loopback_addr(41001),
 959:             peer_public_key([1; 32]),
 960:             vec!["100.64.11.0/24"],
 961:         );
 962:         let peer_two = peer_config(
 963:             "peer-two",
 964:             backend_loopback_addr(41002),
 965:             peer_public_key([2; 32]),
 966:             vec!["100.64.12.0/24"],
 967:         );
 968: 
 969:         backend
 970:             .start(runtime_context())
 971:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
 972:         backend
 973:             .configure_peer(peer_one.clone())
 974:             .expect("first peer configure should succeed");
 975: 
 976:         tun_state.set_next_recv_error("phase10 simulated TUN receive failure");
 977:         wait_for(Duration::from_secs(1), || {
 978:             (backend.current_peer_endpoint(&peer_one.node_id).is_err()).then_some(())
 979:         });
 980: 
 981:         backend
 982:             .configure_peer(peer_two.clone())
 983:             .expect("backend should recover and configure the new peer");
 984: 
 985:         let snapshot = tun_state.snapshot();
 986:         assert_eq!(snapshot.prepare_calls, 2);
 987:         assert_eq!(snapshot.cleanup_calls, 1);
 988:         assert_eq!(snapshot.live_handles, 1);
 989:         assert_eq!(
 990:             backend
 991:                 .current_peer_endpoint(&peer_one.node_id)
 992:                 .expect("recovered backend should expose first peer endpoint"),
 993:             Some(peer_one.endpoint)
 994:         );
 995:         assert_eq!(
 996:             backend
 997:                 .current_peer_endpoint(&peer_two.node_id)
 998:                 .expect("recovered backend should expose second peer endpoint"),
 999:             Some(peer_two.endpoint)
1000:         );
1001: 
1002:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1003:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1004:     }

1007:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_recovers_dead_worker_before_route_reconcile() {
1008:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([14; 32]);
1009:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1010:         let tun_lifecycle = TestTunLifecycle::new();
1011:         let tun_state = tun_lifecycle.state();
1012:         let mut backend =
1013:             backend_with_test_tun_lifecycle(private_key_path.as_path(), listen_port, tun_lifecycle);
1014:         let mesh_route = route("100.64.130.0/24", RouteKind::Mesh);
1015: 
1016:         backend
1017:             .start(runtime_context())
1018:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1019:         tun_state.set_next_recv_error("phase10 simulated TUN receive failure before route apply");
1020:         wait_for(Duration::from_secs(1), || {
1021:             backend
1022:                 .transport_generation_for_test()
1023:                 .ok()
1024:                 .flatten()
1025:                 .and_then(|_| backend.worker_local_addr_for_test().err())
1026:                 .map(|_| ())
1027:         });
1028: 
1029:         backend
1030:             .apply_routes(vec![mesh_route.clone()])
1031:             .expect("backend should recover and replay routes");
1032: 
1033:         let snapshot = tun_state.snapshot();
1034:         assert_eq!(snapshot.prepare_calls, 2);
1035:         assert_eq!(snapshot.cleanup_calls, 1);
1036:         assert_eq!(snapshot.live_handles, 1);
1037:         assert_eq!(
1038:             snapshot.programmed_route_cidrs,
1039:             vec![mesh_route.destination_cidr]
1040:         );
1041: 
1042:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1043:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1044:     }

1047:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_apply_routes_reconciles_non_default_routes_and_preserves_transport_identity()
1048:      {
1049:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([40; 32]);
1050:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1051:         let tun_lifecycle = TestTunLifecycle::new();
1052:         let tun_state = tun_lifecycle.state();
1053:         let mut backend =
1054:             backend_with_test_tun_lifecycle(private_key_path.as_path(), listen_port, tun_lifecycle);
1055:         let mesh_route = route("100.64.20.0/24", RouteKind::Mesh);
1056:         let exit_default = route("0.0.0.0/0", RouteKind::ExitNodeDefault);
1057: 
1058:         backend
1059:             .start(runtime_context())
1060:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1061:         let identity_before = backend
1062:             .authoritative_transport_identity()
1063:             .expect("identity should exist after start");
1064:         let generation_before = backend
1065:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1066:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
1067:             .expect("generation should exist");
1068: 
1069:         backend
1070:             .apply_routes(vec![mesh_route.clone(), exit_default])
1071:             .expect("route reconciliation should succeed");
1072: 
1073:         let identity_after = backend
1074:             .authoritative_transport_identity()
1075:             .expect("identity should remain available after route reconciliation");
1076:         let generation_after = backend
1077:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1078:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
1079:             .expect("generation should exist");
1080:         let snapshot = tun_state.snapshot();
1081: 
1082:         assert_eq!(identity_before, identity_after);
1083:         assert_eq!(generation_before, generation_after);
1084:         assert_eq!(snapshot.route_reconcile_calls, 1);
1085:         assert_eq!(
1086:             snapshot.last_route_interface_name,
1087:             Some("rustynet0".to_string())
1088:         );
1089:         assert_eq!(
1090:             snapshot.programmed_route_cidrs,
1091:             vec!["100.64.20.0/24".to_string()]
1092:         );
1093:         assert_eq!(
1094:             snapshot.route_mutations,
1095:             vec![TunRouteMutation {
1096:                 kind: TunRouteMutationKind::Replace,
1097:                 destination_cidr: "100.64.20.0/24".to_string(),
1098:             }]
1099:         );
1100: 
1101:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1102:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1103:     }

1105:     #[test]
1106:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_apply_routes_removes_stale_routes() {
1107:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([41; 32]);
1108:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1109:         let tun_lifecycle = TestTunLifecycle::new();
1110:         let tun_state = tun_lifecycle.state();
1111:         let mut backend =
1112:             backend_with_test_tun_lifecycle(private_key_path.as_path(), listen_port, tun_lifecycle);
1113:         let first_mesh = route("100.64.30.0/24", RouteKind::Mesh);
1114:         let retained_exit_lan = route("192.168.50.0/24", RouteKind::ExitNodeLan);
1115:         let replacement_mesh = route("100.64.40.0/24", RouteKind::Mesh);
1116: 
1117:         backend
1118:             .start(runtime_context())
1119:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1120:         backend
1121:             .apply_routes(vec![first_mesh.clone(), retained_exit_lan.clone()])
1122:             .expect("initial route apply should succeed");
1123:         backend
1124:             .apply_routes(vec![retained_exit_lan.clone(), replacement_mesh.clone()])
1125:             .expect("route update should succeed");
1126: 
1127:         let snapshot = tun_state.snapshot();
1128:         assert_eq!(snapshot.route_reconcile_calls, 2);
1129:         assert_eq!(
1130:             snapshot.programmed_route_cidrs,
1131:             vec!["192.168.50.0/24".to_string(), "100.64.40.0/24".to_string(),]
1132:         );
1133:         assert!(
1134:             snapshot.route_mutations.iter().any(|mutation| {
1135:                 mutation.kind == TunRouteMutationKind::Delete
1136:                     && mutation.destination_cidr == "100.64.30.0/24"
1137:             }),
1138:             "stale backend-programmed routes must be removed during reconciliation"
1139:         );
1140: 
1141:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1142:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1143:     }

1145:     #[test]
1146:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_apply_routes_failure_rolls_back_without_runtime_state_drift()
1147:     {
1148:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([42; 32]);
1149:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1150:         let tun_lifecycle = TestTunLifecycle::new();
1151:         let tun_state = tun_lifecycle.state();
1152:         let mut backend =
1153:             backend_with_test_tun_lifecycle(private_key_path.as_path(), listen_port, tun_lifecycle);
1154:         let stable_route = route("100.64.60.0/24", RouteKind::Mesh);
1155:         let failing_route = route("100.64.70.0/24", RouteKind::Mesh);
1156: 
1157:         backend
1158:             .start(runtime_context())
1159:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1160:         backend
1161:             .apply_routes(vec![stable_route.clone()])
1162:             .expect("initial route apply should succeed");
1163: 
1164:         tun_state.set_route_behavior(TestRouteBehavior::FailOnReplace {
1165:             cidr: failing_route.destination_cidr.clone(),
1166:             message: "phase1 route replace failure".to_string(),
1167:         });
1168:         let err = backend
1169:             .apply_routes(vec![failing_route.clone()])
1170:             .expect_err("route apply should fail closed when helper route programming fails");
1171:         assert_eq!(err.kind, BackendErrorKind::Internal);
1172:         assert!(err.message.contains("phase1 route replace failure"));
1173: 
1174:         let snapshot_after_failure = tun_state.snapshot();
1175:         assert_eq!(
1176:             snapshot_after_failure.programmed_route_cidrs,
1177:             vec!["100.64.60.0/24".to_string()]
1178:         );
1179: 
1180:         tun_state.set_route_behavior(TestRouteBehavior::Succeed);
1181:         backend.apply_routes(vec![failing_route.clone()]).expect(
1182:             "later route reconciliation should still use the last successful backend state",
1183:         );
1184: 
1185:         let snapshot_after_recovery = tun_state.snapshot();
1186:         assert_eq!(
1187:             snapshot_after_recovery.programmed_route_cidrs,
1188:             vec!["100.64.70.0/24".to_string()]
1189:         );
1190:         assert!(
1191:             snapshot_after_recovery
1192:                 .route_mutations
1193:                 .iter()
1194:                 .any(|mutation| {
1195:                     mutation.kind == TunRouteMutationKind::Delete
1196:                         && mutation.destination_cidr == "100.64.60.0/24"
1197:                 }),
1198:             "the backend must retain the previous successful route set until recovery succeeds"
1199:         );
1200: 
1201:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1202:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1203:     }

1271:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_set_exit_mode_full_tunnel_programs_backend_exit_state() {
1272:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([45; 32]);
1273:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1274:         let tun_lifecycle = TestTunLifecycle::new();
1275:         let tun_state = tun_lifecycle.state();
1276:         let mut backend =
1277:             backend_with_test_tun_lifecycle(private_key_path.as_path(), listen_port, tun_lifecycle);
1278: 
1279:         backend
1280:             .start(runtime_context())
1281:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1282:         let identity_before = backend
1283:             .authoritative_transport_identity()
1284:             .expect("identity should exist after start");
1285:         let generation_before = backend
1286:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1287:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
1288:             .expect("generation should exist");
1289: 
1290:         backend
1291:             .set_exit_mode(ExitMode::FullTunnel)
1292:             .expect("full tunnel exit mode should apply");
1293: 
1294:         let identity_after = backend
1295:             .authoritative_transport_identity()
1296:             .expect("identity should remain available after exit-mode apply");
1297:         let generation_after = backend
1298:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1299:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
1300:             .expect("generation should exist");
1301:         let snapshot = tun_state.snapshot();
1302: 
1303:         assert_eq!(identity_before, identity_after);
1304:         assert_eq!(generation_before, generation_after);
1305:         assert_eq!(snapshot.exit_mode_reconcile_calls, 1);
1306:         assert_eq!(snapshot.last_exit_mode_target, Some(ExitMode::FullTunnel));
1307:         assert_eq!(snapshot.current_exit_mode, ExitMode::FullTunnel);
1308:         assert_eq!(
1309:             snapshot.exit_mode_mutations,
1310:             vec![
1311:                 TunExitModeMutation::DeleteTable,
1312:                 TunExitModeMutation::DeletePriority,
1313:                 TunExitModeMutation::AddPriority,
1314:             ]
1315:         );
1316: 
1317:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1318:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1319:     }

1573:     #[test]
1574:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_second_concurrent_round_trip_is_rejected() {
1575:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([16; 32]);
1576:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1577:         let remote_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
1578:             .expect("remote socket should bind");
1579:         let remote_addr = remote_socket
1580:             .local_addr()
1581:             .expect("remote addr should resolve");
1582:         let (request_seen_tx, request_seen_rx) = mpsc::channel();
1583:         let responder = thread::spawn(move || {
1584:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
1585:             let (len, source) = remote_socket
1586:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
1587:                 .expect("first round-trip request should arrive");
1588:             request_seen_tx
1589:                 .send(())
1590:                 .expect("request signal should be sent");
1591:             thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(150));
1592:             remote_socket
1593:                 .send_to(&buf[..len], source)
1594:                 .expect("response should be sent");
1595:         });
1596: 
1597:         let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
1598:             "rustynet0",
1599:             private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
1600:             listen_port,
1601:         )
1602:         .expect("backend should construct");
1603:         backend
1604:             .start(runtime_context())
1605:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1606: 
1607:         let control = backend
1608:             .runtime
1609:             .as_ref()
1610:             .expect("runtime should exist")
1611:             .control()
1612:             .clone();
1613:         let first_control = control.clone();
1614:         let first_round_trip = thread::spawn(move || {
1615:             first_control.authoritative_round_trip(
1616:                 remote_addr,
1617:                 b"first".to_vec(),
1618:                 Duration::from_secs(1),
1619:             )
1620:         });
1621: 
1622:         request_seen_rx
1623:             .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(1))
1624:             .expect("first round-trip request should be observed");
1625: 
1626:         let err = control
1627:             .authoritative_round_trip(remote_addr, b"second".to_vec(), Duration::from_secs(1))
1628:             .expect_err("second concurrent round trip should be rejected");
1629:         assert_eq!(err.kind, BackendErrorKind::Internal);
1630:         assert!(err.message.contains("already in flight"));
1631: 
1632:         let response = first_round_trip
1633:             .join()
1634:             .expect("first round-trip thread should join")
1635:             .expect("first round trip should succeed");
1636:         assert_eq!(response.remote_addr, remote_addr);
1637:         assert_eq!(
1638:             response.local_addr,
1639:             SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port))
1640:         );
1641: 
1642:         responder.join().expect("responder should join");
1643:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1644:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1645:     }

1647:     #[test]
1648:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_stun_round_trip_uses_same_transport_generation_as_peer_path()
1649:     {
1650:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([17; 32]);
1651:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1652:         let authoritative_addr = backend_loopback_addr(listen_port);
1653:         let peer_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
1654:             .expect("peer socket should bind");
1655:         let peer_addr = peer_socket.local_addr().expect("peer addr should resolve");
1656:         let stun_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
1657:             .expect("stun socket should bind");
1658:         let stun_addr = stun_socket.local_addr().expect("stun addr should resolve");
1659:         let stun_responder = thread::spawn(move || {
1660:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
1661:             let (_len, source) = stun_socket
1662:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
1663:                 .expect("stun request should arrive");
1664:             stun_socket
1665:                 .send_to(b"stun-response", source)
1666:                 .expect("stun response should be sent");
1667:         });
1668: 
1669:         let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
1670:             "rustynet0",
1671:             private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
1672:             listen_port,
1673:         )
1674:         .expect("backend should construct");
1675:         backend
1676:             .start(runtime_context())
1677:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1678:         backend
1679:             .configure_peer(sample_peer("peer-a", peer_addr))
1680:             .expect("peer should configure");
1681: 
1682:         let generation = backend
1683:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1684:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
1685:             .expect("generation should exist");
1686:         peer_socket
1687:             .send_to(b"peer-ciphertext", authoritative_addr)
1688:             .expect("peer ciphertext should be sent");
1689:         let peer_ingress = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(1), || {
1690:             let records = backend
1691:                 .recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress_for_test()
1692:                 .expect("peer ingress query should succeed");
1693:             (records.len() == 1).then_some(records)
1694:         });
1695: 
1696:         let response = backend
1697:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(stun_addr, b"stun-request", Duration::from_secs(1))
1698:             .expect("stun round trip should succeed");
1699:         let operations = backend
1700:             .recorded_authoritative_transport_operations_for_test()
1701:             .expect("authoritative operations should be queryable");
1702: 
1703:         assert_eq!(response.remote_addr, stun_addr);
1704:         assert_eq!(
1705:             response.local_addr,
1706:             SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port))
1707:         );
1708:         assert_eq!(response.payload, b"stun-response");
1709:         assert_eq!(peer_ingress[0].remote_addr, peer_addr);
1710:         assert_eq!(
1711:             peer_ingress[0].local_addr,
1712:             SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port))
1713:         );
1714:         assert_eq!(peer_ingress[0].transport_generation, generation);
1715:         assert_eq!(operations.len(), 1);
1716:         assert_eq!(
1717:             operations[0].kind,
1718:             RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::RoundTrip
1719:         );
1720:         assert_eq!(operations[0].remote_addr, stun_addr);
1721:         assert_eq!(
1722:             operations[0].local_addr,
1723:             SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port))
1724:         );
1725:         assert_eq!(operations[0].transport_generation, generation);
1726: 
1727:         stun_responder.join().expect("stun responder should join");
1728:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1729:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1730:     }
