1732:     #[test]
1733:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_relay_round_trip_and_send_use_same_transport_generation_as_peer_path()
1734:      {
1735:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([18; 32]);
1736:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
1737:         let authoritative_addr = backend_loopback_addr(listen_port);
1738:         let peer_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
1739:             .expect("peer socket should bind");
1740:         let peer_addr = peer_socket.local_addr().expect("peer addr should resolve");
1741:         let relay_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
1742:             .expect("relay socket should bind");
1743:         let relay_addr = relay_socket
1744:             .local_addr()
1745:             .expect("relay addr should resolve");
1746:         let (keepalive_tx, keepalive_rx) = mpsc::channel();
1747:         let relay_worker = thread::spawn(move || {
1748:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
1749:             let (_len, source) = relay_socket
1750:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
1751:                 .expect("relay hello should arrive");
1752:             relay_socket
1753:                 .send_to(b"relay-ack", source)
1754:                 .expect("relay ack should be sent");
1755:             let (len, source) = relay_socket
1756:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
1757:                 .expect("relay keepalive should arrive");
1758:             keepalive_tx
1759:                 .send((buf[..len].to_vec(), source))
1760:                 .expect("keepalive should be recorded");
1761:         });
1762: 
1763:         let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
1764:             "rustynet0",
1765:             private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
1766:             listen_port,
1767:         )
1768:         .expect("backend should construct");
1769:         backend
1770:             .start(runtime_context())
1771:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
1772:         backend
1773:             .configure_peer(sample_peer("peer-a", peer_addr))
1774:             .expect("peer should configure");
1775: 
1776:         let generation = backend
1777:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1778:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
1779:             .expect("generation should exist");
1780:         peer_socket
1781:             .send_to(b"peer-ciphertext", authoritative_addr)
1782:             .expect("peer ciphertext should be sent");
1783:         let _ = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(1), || {
1784:             let records = backend
1785:                 .recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress_for_test()
1786:                 .expect("peer ingress query should succeed");
1787:             (records.len() == 1).then_some(records)
1788:         });
1789: 
1790:         let response = backend
1791:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(relay_addr, b"relay-hello", Duration::from_secs(1))
1792:             .expect("relay round trip should succeed");
1793:         let identity = backend
1794:             .authoritative_transport_send(relay_addr, b"relay-keepalive")
1795:             .expect("relay keepalive send should succeed");
1796:         let operations = backend
1797:             .recorded_authoritative_transport_operations_for_test()
1798:             .expect("authoritative operations should be queryable");
1799:         let (keepalive_payload, observed_source) = keepalive_rx
1800:             .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(1))
1801:             .expect("keepalive should be observed");
1802: 
1803:         assert_eq!(response.payload, b"relay-ack");
1804:         assert_eq!(
1805:             identity.local_addr,
1806:             SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port))
1807:         );
1808:         assert_eq!(keepalive_payload, b"relay-keepalive");
1809:         assert_eq!(observed_source.port(), listen_port);
1810:         assert_eq!(operations.len(), 2);
1811:         assert_eq!(
1812:             operations[0].kind,
1813:             RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::RoundTrip
1814:         );
1815:         assert_eq!(operations[0].transport_generation, generation);
1816:         assert_eq!(
1817:             operations[1].kind,
1818:             RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::Send
1819:         );
1820:         assert_eq!(operations[1].transport_generation, generation);
1821: 
1822:         relay_worker.join().expect("relay worker should join");
1823:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
1824:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
1825:     }
1826: 
1827:     #[test]
1828:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_multi_peer_simulated_proof_uses_one_generation_for_peer_stun_and_relay_paths()
1829:      {
1830:         let left_private_key = [36; 32];
1831:         let right_private_key = [37; 32];
1832:         let left_private_key_path = write_private_key(left_private_key);
1833:         let right_private_key_path = write_private_key(right_private_key);
1834:         let left_port = free_listen_port();
1835:         let right_port = free_listen_port();
1836:         let left_identity_addr = SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], left_port));
1837:         let stun_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
1838:             .expect("stun socket should bind");
1839:         let stun_addr = stun_socket.local_addr().expect("stun addr should resolve");
1840:         let stun_worker = thread::spawn(move || {
1841:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
1842:             let (_len, source) = stun_socket
1843:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
1844:                 .expect("stun request should arrive");
1845:             stun_socket
1846:                 .send_to(b"stun-proof-response", source)
1847:                 .expect("stun response should be sent");
1848:         });
1849:         let relay_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
1850:             .expect("relay socket should bind");
1851:         let relay_addr = relay_socket
1852:             .local_addr()
1853:             .expect("relay addr should resolve");
1854:         let (keepalive_tx, keepalive_rx) = mpsc::channel();
1855:         let relay_worker = thread::spawn(move || {
1856:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
1857:             let (_len, source) = relay_socket
1858:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
1859:                 .expect("relay hello should arrive");
1860:             relay_socket
1861:                 .send_to(b"relay-proof-ack", source)
1862:                 .expect("relay ack should be sent");
1863:             let (len, source) = relay_socket
1864:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
1865:                 .expect("relay keepalive should arrive");
1866:             keepalive_tx
1867:                 .send((buf[..len].to_vec(), source))
1868:                 .expect("keepalive should be recorded");
1869:         });
1870: 
1871:         let mut left_backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
1872:             "rustynet0",
1873:             left_private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
1874:             left_port,
1875:         )
1876:         .expect("left backend should construct");
1877:         let mut right_backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
1878:             "rustynet1",
1879:             right_private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
1880:             right_port,
1881:         )
1882:         .expect("right backend should construct");
1883:         left_backend
1884:             .start(runtime_context())
1885:             .expect("left backend should start");
1886:         right_backend
1887:             .start(RuntimeContext {
1888:                 local_node: NodeId::new("phase6-right").expect("valid node id"),
1889:                 interface_name: "rustynet1".to_string(),
1890:                 mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),
1891:                 local_cidr: "100.64.1.1/32".to_string(),
1892:             })
1893:             .expect("right backend should start");
1894: 
1895:         left_backend
1896:             .configure_peer(peer_config(
1897:                 "peer-right",
1898:                 backend_loopback_addr(right_port),
1899:                 peer_public_key(right_private_key),
1900:                 vec!["100.64.2.0/24"],
1901:             ))
1902:             .expect("left peer configure should succeed");
1903:         right_backend
1904:             .configure_peer(peer_config(
1905:                 "peer-left",
1906:                 backend_loopback_addr(left_port),
1907:                 peer_public_key(left_private_key),
1908:                 vec!["100.64.1.0/24"],
1909:             ))
1910:             .expect("right peer configure should succeed");
1911: 
1912:         let left_generation = left_backend
1913:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1914:             .expect("left generation query should succeed")
1915:             .expect("left generation should exist");
1916:         let right_generation = right_backend
1917:             .transport_generation_for_test()
1918:             .expect("right generation query should succeed")
1919:             .expect("right generation should exist");
1920:         assert_ne!(left_generation, right_generation);
1921: 
1922:         let simulated_plaintext = build_ipv4_udp_packet(
1923:             Ipv4Addr::new(100, 64, 1, 10),
1924:             Ipv4Addr::new(100, 64, 2, 20),
1925:             b"phase6-simulated-proof",
1926:         );
1927:         left_backend
1928:             .queue_tun_plaintext_packet_for_test(simulated_plaintext.clone())
1929:             .expect("test TUN plaintext queue should succeed");
1930: 
1931:         let left_peer_egress = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(2), || {
1932:             let records = left_backend
1933:                 .recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress_for_test()
1934:                 .expect("left peer egress query should succeed");
1935:             (!records.is_empty()).then_some(records)
1936:         });
1937:         let right_plaintext_packets = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(2), || {
1938:             let packets = right_backend
1939:                 .recorded_tun_outbound_packets_for_test()
1940:                 .expect("right test TUN outbound packet query should succeed");
1941:             (!packets.is_empty()).then_some(packets)
1942:         });
1943: 
1944:         let stun_response = left_backend
1945:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(
1946:                 stun_addr,
1947:                 b"stun-proof-request",
1948:                 Duration::from_secs(1),
1949:             )
1950:             .expect("stun round trip should succeed");
1951:         let relay_response = left_backend
1952:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(
1953:                 relay_addr,
1954:                 b"relay-proof-hello",
1955:                 Duration::from_secs(1),
1956:             )
1957:             .expect("relay round trip should succeed");
1958:         let relay_identity = left_backend
1959:             .authoritative_transport_send(relay_addr, b"relay-proof-keepalive")
1960:             .expect("relay keepalive send should succeed");
1961:         let authoritative_operations = left_backend
1962:             .recorded_authoritative_transport_operations_for_test()
1963:             .expect("authoritative operations should be queryable");
1964:         let (keepalive_payload, keepalive_source) = keepalive_rx
1965:             .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(1))
1966:             .expect("relay keepalive should be observed");
1967: 
1968:         assert_eq!(stun_response.payload, b"stun-proof-response");
1969:         assert_eq!(relay_response.payload, b"relay-proof-ack");
1970:         assert_eq!(relay_identity.local_addr, left_identity_addr);
1971:         assert_eq!(keepalive_payload, b"relay-proof-keepalive");
1972:         assert_eq!(keepalive_source.port(), left_identity_addr.port());
1973:         assert!(left_peer_egress.iter().all(|record| record.remote_addr
1974:             == backend_loopback_addr(right_port)
1975:             && record.local_addr == left_identity_addr
1976:             && record.transport_generation == left_generation));
1977:         assert_eq!(
1978:             right_plaintext_packets,
1979:             vec![simulated_plaintext],
1980:             "the receiving backend must write the decrypted packet to its owned TUN device"
1981:         );
1982:         assert_eq!(authoritative_operations.len(), 3);
1983:         assert_eq!(
1984:             authoritative_operations[0].kind,
1985:             RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::RoundTrip
1986:         );
1987:         assert_eq!(authoritative_operations[0].remote_addr, stun_addr);
1988:         assert_eq!(
1989:             authoritative_operations[1].kind,
1990:             RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::RoundTrip
1991:         );
1992:         assert_eq!(authoritative_operations[1].remote_addr, relay_addr);
1993:         assert_eq!(
1994:             authoritative_operations[2].kind,
1995:             RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::Send
1996:         );
1997:         assert_eq!(authoritative_operations[2].remote_addr, relay_addr);
1998:         assert!(
1999:             authoritative_operations
2000:                 .iter()
2001:                 .all(|record| record.local_addr == left_identity_addr
2002:                     && record.transport_generation == left_generation)
2003:         );
2004: 
2005:         let path_generations = left_peer_egress
2006:             .iter()
2007:             .map(|record| record.transport_generation)
2008:             .chain(
2009:                 authoritative_operations
2010:                     .iter()
2011:                     .map(|record| record.transport_generation),
2012:             )
2013:             .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2014:         let path_local_addrs = left_peer_egress
2015:             .iter()
2016:             .map(|record| record.local_addr)
2017:             .chain(
2018:                 authoritative_operations
2019:                     .iter()
2020:                     .map(|record| record.local_addr),
2021:             )
2022:             .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
2023: 
2024:         assert_eq!(
2025:             path_generations,
2026:             BTreeSet::from([left_generation]),
2027:             "peer ciphertext, STUN, and relay control must all traverse the same authoritative transport generation"
2028:         );
2029:         assert_eq!(
2030:             path_local_addrs,
2031:             BTreeSet::from([left_identity_addr]),
2032:             "the simulated proof must not rely on any second authority socket in the left backend path"
2033:         );
2034: 
2035:         stun_worker.join().expect("stun worker should join");
2036:         relay_worker.join().expect("relay worker should join");
2037:         left_backend
2038:             .shutdown()
2039:             .expect("left shutdown should succeed");
2040:         right_backend
2041:             .shutdown()
2042:             .expect("right shutdown should succeed");
2043:         let _ = fs::remove_file(left_private_key_path);
2044:         let _ = fs::remove_file(right_private_key_path);
2045:     }

2154:         );

2300:                 .expect("left handshake query should succeed"),

2302:         );
2303:         assert_eq!(
2304:             right_backend
2305:                 .peer_latest_handshake_unix(&right_peer_node)
2306:                 .expect("right handshake query should succeed"),
2307:             None
2308:         );
2309: 
2310:         let plaintext_packet = build_ipv4_udp_packet(

2313:             b"phase4-engine-payload",
2314:         );

2316:             .inject_plaintext_packet_for_test(plaintext_packet.clone())
2317:             .expect("plaintext injection should succeed");
2318:

2320:             let packets = right_backend
2321:                 .recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets_for_test()
2322:                 .expect("tunnel packet query should succeed");
2323:             (packets.len() == 1).then_some(packets)
2324:         });

2326:             left_backend

2328:                 .expect("left handshake query should succeed")
2329:         });

2333:                 .expect("right handshake query should succeed")
2334:         });
2335: 
2336:         assert_eq!(delivered_packets[0].node_id, right_peer_node);
2337:         assert_eq!(delivered_packets[0].packet, plaintext_packet);
2338:         assert!(left_handshake > 0);
2339:         assert!(right_handshake > 0);
2340: 
2341:         let left_stats = left_backend.stats().expect("left stats should succeed");
2342:         let right_stats = right_backend.stats().expect("right stats should succeed");
2343:         assert_eq!(left_stats.peer_count, 1);
2344:         assert_eq!(right_stats.peer_count, 1);
2345:         assert!(left_stats.bytes_tx >= plaintext_packet.len() as u64);
2346:         assert!(right_stats.bytes_rx >= plaintext_packet.len() as u64);
2347:         assert!(!left_stats.using_relay_path);
2348:         assert!(!right_stats.using_relay_path);
2349: 
2350:         left_backend
2351:             .shutdown()
2352:             .expect("left shutdown should succeed");
2353:         right_backend
2354:             .shutdown()
2355:             .expect("right shutdown should succeed");
2356:         let _ = fs::remove_file(left_private_key_path);
2357:         let _ = fs::remove_file(right_private_key_path);
2358:     }

2395:             ))
2396:             .expect("left peer configure should succeed");
2397:         right_backend
2398:             .configure_peer(peer_config(
2399:                 "peer-left",
2400:                 backend_loopback_addr(left_port),
2401:                 peer_public_key(left_private_key),
2402:                 vec!["100.64.1.0/24"],
2403:             ))
2404:             .expect("right peer configure should succeed");
2405: 
2406:         let left_generation = left_backend
2407:             .transport_generation_for_test()
2408:             .expect("left generation query should succeed")
2409:             .expect("left generation should exist");
2410:         let right_generation = right_backend
2411:             .transport_generation_for_test()
2412:             .expect("right generation query should succeed")
2413:             .expect("right generation should exist");
2414: 
2415:         left_backend
2416:             .initiate_peer_handshake(&left_peer_node, false)
2417:             .expect("left handshake initiation should succeed");
2418: 
2419:         let left_egress = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(2), || {
2420:             let records = left_backend
2421:                 .recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress_for_test()
2422:                 .expect("left peer egress query should succeed");
2423:             (!records.is_empty()).then_some(records)
2424:         });
2425:         let right_ingress = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(2), || {
2426:             let records = right_backend
2427:                 .recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress_for_test()
2428:                 .expect("right peer ingress query should succeed");
2429:             (!records.is_empty()).then_some(records)
2430:         });
2431:         let left_handshake = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(2), || {
2432:             left_backend
2433:                 .peer_latest_handshake_unix(&left_peer_node)
2434:                 .expect("left handshake query should succeed")
2435:         });
2436:         let right_handshake = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(2), || {
2437:             right_backend
2438:                 .peer_latest_handshake_unix(&right_peer_node)
2439:                 .expect("right handshake query should succeed")
2440:         });
2441: 
2442:         assert_eq!(left_egress[0].transport_generation, left_generation);
2443:         assert_eq!(right_ingress[0].transport_generation, right_generation);
2444:         assert_eq!(
2445:             left_egress[0].remote_addr,
2446:             SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], right_port))
2447:         );
2448:         assert_eq!(
2449:             right_ingress[0].remote_addr,
2450:             SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], left_port))
2451:         );
2452:         assert!(left_handshake > 0);
2453:         assert!(right_handshake > 0);
2454: 
2455:         left_backend
2456:             .shutdown()
2457:             .expect("left shutdown should succeed");
2458:         right_backend
2459:             .shutdown()
2460:             .expect("right shutdown should succeed");
2461:         let _ = fs::remove_file(left_private_key_path);
2462:         let _ = fs::remove_file(right_private_key_path);
2463:     }

2467:      {
2468:         let left_private_key = [33; 32];
2469:         let right_private_key = [34; 32];
2470:         let left_private_key_path = write_private_key(left_private_key);
2471:         let right_private_key_path = write_private_key(right_private_key);
2472:         let left_port = free_listen_port();

2475:             "rustynet0",
2476:             left_private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
2477:             left_port,
2478:         )
2479:         .expect("left backend should construct");
2480:         let mut right_backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
2481:             "rustynet1",
2482:             right_private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
2483:             right_port,
2484:         )
2485:         .expect("right backend should construct");

2487:             .start(runtime_context())
2488:             .expect("left backend should start");

2490:             .start(RuntimeContext {
2491:                 local_node: NodeId::new("phase4-right").expect("valid node id"),
2492:                 interface_name: "rustynet1".to_string(),
2493:                 mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),
2494:                 local_cidr: "100.64.1.1/32".to_string(),
2495:             })
2496:             .expect("right backend should start");
2497: 
2498:         left_backend
2499:             .configure_peer(peer_config(
2500:                 "peer-right",
2501:                 backend_loopback_addr(right_port),
2502:                 peer_public_key(right_private_key),
2503:                 vec!["100.64.2.0/24"],
2504:             ))
2505:             .expect("left peer configure should succeed");
2506:         right_backend
2507:             .configure_peer(peer_config(
2508:                 "peer-left",
2509:                 backend_loopback_addr(left_port),
2510:                 peer_public_key(left_private_key),
2511:                 vec!["100.64.1.0/24"],
2512:             ))
2513:             .expect("right peer configure should succeed");
2514: 
2515:         let ignored_plaintext = build_ipv4_udp_packet(
2516:             Ipv4Addr::new(100, 64, 1, 10),
2517:             Ipv4Addr::new(198, 51, 100, 25),
2518:             b"phase7-ignored-unmatched",
2519:         );
2520:         let valid_plaintext = build_ipv4_udp_packet(
2521:             Ipv4Addr::new(100, 64, 1, 10),
2522:             Ipv4Addr::new(100, 64, 2, 20),
2523:             b"phase7-valid-after-unmatched",
2524:         );

2526:             .queue_tun_plaintext_packet_for_test(ignored_plaintext)
2527:             .expect("ignored plaintext queue should succeed");
2528:         left_backend
2529:             .queue_tun_plaintext_packet_for_test(valid_plaintext.clone())
2530:             .expect("valid plaintext queue should succeed");
2531: 
2532:         let delivered_packets = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(2), || {
2533:             let packets = right_backend
2534:                 .recorded_tun_outbound_packets_for_test()
2535:                 .expect("right outbound packet query should succeed");
2536:             (packets.len() == 1).then_some(packets)
2537:         });
2538: 
2539:         assert_eq!(delivered_packets, vec![valid_plaintext]);
2540:         assert_eq!(left_backend.worker_exit_count_for_test(), Some(0));
2541:         assert_eq!(
2542:             left_backend
2543:                 .stats()
2544:                 .expect("left stats should succeed")
2545:                 .peer_count,
2546:             1
2547:         );

2550:             .shutdown()

2553:             .shutdown()

2649:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_stun_round_trip_does_not_advance_peer_handshake() {
2650:         let local_private_key = write_private_key([28; 32]);
2651:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
2652:         let stun_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
2653:             .expect("stun socket should bind");
2654:         let stun_addr = stun_socket.local_addr().expect("stun addr should resolve");
2655:         let responder = thread::spawn(move || {
2656:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
2657:             let (_len, source) = stun_socket
2658:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
2659:                 .expect("stun request should arrive");
2660:             stun_socket
2661:                 .send_to(b"stun-ok", source)
2662:                 .expect("stun response should be sent");
2663:         });
2664:         let peer_node = NodeId::new("peer-a").expect("valid node id");
2665:         let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
2666:             "rustynet0",
2667:             local_private_key.to_string_lossy(),
2668:             listen_port,
2669:         )
2670:         .expect("backend should construct");
2671:         backend
2672:             .start(runtime_context())
2673:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
2674:         backend
2675:             .configure_peer(peer_config(
2676:                 "peer-a",
2677:                 SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], free_listen_port())),
2678:                 peer_public_key([29; 32]),
2679:                 vec!["100.64.2.0/24"],
2680:             ))
2681:             .expect("peer configure should succeed");
2682: 
2683:         let response = backend
2684:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(stun_addr, b"stun-probe", Duration::from_secs(1))
2685:             .expect("stun round trip should succeed");
2686:         assert_eq!(response.payload, b"stun-ok");
2687:         assert_eq!(
2688:             backend
2689:                 .peer_latest_handshake_unix(&peer_node)
2690:                 .expect("handshake query should succeed"),
2691:             None
2692:         );
2693: 
2694:         responder.join().expect("stun responder should join");
2695:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
2696:         let _ = fs::remove_file(local_private_key);
2697:     }

2929:     #[test]
2930:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_restart_cancels_stale_round_trip_and_same_port_new_socket_does_not_reuse_it()
2931:      {
2932:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([38; 32]);
2933:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
2934:         let stale_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
2935:             .expect("stale remote socket should bind");
2936:         let stale_addr = stale_socket
2937:             .local_addr()
2938:             .expect("stale remote addr should resolve");
2939:         let (request_seen_tx, request_seen_rx) = mpsc::channel();
2940:         let (send_late_tx, send_late_rx) = mpsc::channel();
2941:         let stale_worker = thread::spawn(move || {
2942:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
2943:             let (_len, source) = stale_socket
2944:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
2945:                 .expect("initial stale round-trip request should arrive");
2946:             request_seen_tx
2947:                 .send(source)
2948:                 .expect("stale request signal should be sent");
2949:             send_late_rx
2950:                 .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2))
2951:                 .expect("late-send signal should arrive");
2952:             stale_socket
2953:                 .send_to(b"late-after-rollover", source)
2954:                 .expect("late response should be sent");
2955:         });
2956:         let prompt_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
2957:             .expect("prompt remote socket should bind");
2958:         let prompt_addr = prompt_socket
2959:             .local_addr()
2960:             .expect("prompt remote addr should resolve");
2961:         let prompt_worker = thread::spawn(move || {
2962:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
2963:             let (_len, source) = prompt_socket
2964:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
2965:                 .expect("prompt request should arrive");
2966:             prompt_socket
2967:                 .send_to(b"prompt-after-rollover", source)
2968:                 .expect("prompt response should be sent");
2969:         });
2970: 
2971:         let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
2972:             "rustynet0",
2973:             private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
2974:             listen_port,
2975:         )
2976:         .expect("backend should construct");
2977:         backend
2978:             .start(runtime_context())
2979:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
2980:         let first_identity = backend
2981:             .authoritative_transport_identity()
2982:             .expect("identity should exist after start");
2983:         let first_generation = backend
2984:             .transport_generation_for_test()
2985:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
2986:             .expect("generation should exist");
2987: 
2988:         let control = backend
2989:             .runtime
2990:             .as_ref()
2991:             .expect("runtime should exist")
2992:             .control()
2993:             .clone();
2994:         let first_round_trip = thread::spawn(move || {
2995:             control.authoritative_round_trip(
2996:                 stale_addr,
2997:                 b"first-before-rollover".to_vec(),
2998:                 Duration::from_secs(2),
2999:             )
3000:         });
3001: 
3002:         request_seen_rx
3003:             .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(1))
3004:             .expect("initial stale request should be observed");
3005: 
3006:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
3007:         let canceled_err = first_round_trip
3008:             .join()
3009:             .expect("first round-trip thread should join")
3010:             .expect_err("stale round trip should be canceled during shutdown");
3011:         assert_eq!(canceled_err.kind, BackendErrorKind::Internal);
3012:         assert!(
3013:             canceled_err
3014:                 .message
3015:                 .contains("canceled during backend shutdown")
3016:         );

3018:         backend
3019:             .start(runtime_context())
3020:             .expect("backend should restart successfully");
3021:         let second_identity = backend
3022:             .authoritative_transport_identity()
3023:             .expect("identity should exist after restart");
3024:         let second_generation = backend
3025:             .transport_generation_for_test()
3026:             .expect("generation query should succeed")
3027:             .expect("generation should exist");
3028:         assert_eq!(first_identity.local_addr, second_identity.local_addr);
3029:         assert_ne!(first_generation, second_generation);
3030: 
3031:         send_late_tx
3032:             .send(())
3033:             .expect("late-send signal should be delivered");
3034:         stale_worker.join().expect("stale worker should join");
3035: 
3036:         let stale_ingress = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(1), || {
3037:             let records = backend
3038:                 .recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress_for_test()
3039:                 .expect("peer ingress query should succeed");
3040:             (records.iter().any(|record| {

3042:                     && record.payload == b"late-after-rollover"
3043:                     && record.transport_generation == second_generation
3044:             }))
3045:             .then_some(records)

3049:                 record.remote_addr == stale_addr
3050:                     && record.payload == b"late-after-rollover"
3051:                     && record.transport_generation == second_generation
3052:             }),
3053:             "late packet from the old socket generation must not satisfy the stale waiter"
3054:         );

3070:     #[test]
3071:     fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_timeout_clears_waiter_and_late_packet_becomes_peer_input() {
3072:         let private_key_path = write_private_key([20; 32]);
3073:         let listen_port = free_listen_port();
3074:         let remote_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
3075:             .expect("slow remote socket should bind");
3076:         let remote_addr = remote_socket
3077:             .local_addr()
3078:             .expect("slow remote addr should resolve");
3079:         let slow_responder = thread::spawn(move || {
3080:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
3081:             let (_len, source) = remote_socket
3082:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
3083:                 .expect("slow round-trip request should arrive");
3084:             thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(150));
3085:             remote_socket
3086:                 .send_to(b"late-response", source)
3087:                 .expect("late response should be sent");
3088:         });
3089:         let prompt_socket = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
3090:             .expect("prompt remote socket should bind");
3091:         let prompt_addr = prompt_socket
3092:             .local_addr()
3093:             .expect("prompt remote addr should resolve");
3094:         let prompt_responder = thread::spawn(move || {
3095:             let mut buf = [0u8; 1024];
3096:             let (_len, source) = prompt_socket
3097:                 .recv_from(&mut buf)
3098:                 .expect("prompt round-trip request should arrive");
3099:             prompt_socket
3100:                 .send_to(b"prompt-response", source)
3101:                 .expect("prompt response should be sent");
3102:         });
3103: 
3104:         let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
3105:             "rustynet0",
3106:             private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
3107:             listen_port,
3108:         )
3109:         .expect("backend should construct");
3110:         backend
3111:             .start(runtime_context())
3112:             .expect("backend should start successfully");
3113: 
3114:         let err = backend
3115:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(
3116:                 remote_addr,
3117:                 b"will-timeout",
3118:                 Duration::from_millis(50),
3119:             )
3120:             .expect_err("slow round trip should time out");
3121:         assert_eq!(err.kind, BackendErrorKind::Internal);
3122:         assert!(err.message.contains("timed out"));
3123: 
3124:         slow_responder.join().expect("slow responder should join");
3125:         let peer_ingress = wait_for(Duration::from_secs(1), || {
3126:             let records = backend
3127:                 .recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress_for_test()
3128:                 .expect("peer ingress query should succeed");

3130:                 .iter()
3131:                 .any(|record| record.remote_addr == remote_addr))
3132:             .then_some(records)
3133:         });
3134:         assert!(
3135:             peer_ingress
3136:                 .iter()
3137:                 .any(|record| record.remote_addr == remote_addr
3138:                     && record.payload == b"late-response")
3139:         );
3140: 
3141:         let response = backend
3142:             .authoritative_transport_round_trip(
3143:                 prompt_addr,
3144:                 b"prompt-request",
3145:                 Duration::from_secs(1),
3146:             )
3147:             .expect("next round trip should succeed after timeout cleanup");
3148:         assert_eq!(response.payload, b"prompt-response");
3149: 
3150:         prompt_responder
3151:             .join()
3152:             .expect("prompt responder should join");
3153:         backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
3154:         let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
3155:     }

  50: #[derive(Debug)]
  51: pub(crate) struct RunningUserspaceRuntime {
  52:     control: RuntimeControl,
  53:     join_handle: JoinHandle<()>,
  54: }
  55: 
  56: impl RunningUserspaceRuntime {
  57:     pub(crate) fn start(
  58:         interface_name: &str,
  59:         context: RuntimeContext,
  60:         tun_device: TunDevice,
  61:         authoritative_socket: AuthoritativeSocket,
  62:         engine: UserspaceEngine,
  63:         tun_lifecycle: SharedTunLifecycle,
  64:     ) -> Result<Self, BackendError> {
  65:         let (command_tx, command_rx) = mpsc::channel();
  66:         let (ready_tx, ready_rx) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
  67:         let test_state = RuntimeTestState::default();
  68:         let worker_test_state = test_state.clone();
  69:         let round_trip_in_flight = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
  70:         let thread_name = format!("rustynet-wg-userspace-{interface_name}");
  71: 
  72:         let join_handle = thread::Builder::new()
  73:             .name(thread_name)
  74:             .spawn(move || {
  75:                 run_worker(WorkerRuntimeParts {
  76:                     context,
  77:                     tun_device,
  78:                     authoritative_socket,
  79:                     engine,
  80:                     tun_lifecycle,
  81:                     command_rx,
  82:                     ready_tx,
  83:                     test_state: worker_test_state,
  84:                 });
  85:             })
  86:             .map_err(|err| {
  87:                 BackendError::internal(format!(
  88:                     "linux userspace-shared runtime worker spawn failed: {err}"
  89:                 ))
  90:             })?;
  91: 
  92:         let authoritative_identity = match ready_rx.recv() {
  93:             Ok(Ok(identity)) => identity,
  94:             Ok(Err(err)) => {
  95:                 let _ = join_handle.join();
  96:                 return Err(err);
  97:             }
  98:             Err(_) => {
  99:                 let _ = join_handle.join();
 100:                 return Err(BackendError::internal(
 101:                     "linux userspace-shared runtime worker exited before reporting readiness",
 102:                 ));
 103:             }
 104:         };
 105: 
 106:         Ok(Self {
 107:             control: RuntimeControl {
 108:                 command_tx,
 109:                 authoritative_identity,
 110:                 round_trip_in_flight,
 111:                 test_state,
 112:             },
 113:             join_handle,
 114:         })
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     pub(crate) fn control(&self) -> &RuntimeControl {
 118:         &self.control
 119:     }
 120: 
 121:     pub(crate) fn shutdown(self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 122:         let shutdown_result = self.control.shutdown();
 123:         let join_result = self.join_handle.join().map_err(|_| {
 124:             BackendError::internal("linux userspace-shared runtime worker panicked during shutdown")
 125:         });
 126: 
 127:         match (shutdown_result, join_result) {
 128:             (Ok(()), Ok(())) => Ok(()),
 129:             (Err(err), Ok(())) => Err(err),
 130:             (Ok(()), Err(err)) => Err(err),
 131:             (Err(err), Err(_)) => Err(err),
 132:         }
 133:     }
 134: }
 135: 
 136: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
 137: pub(crate) struct RuntimeControl {
 138:     command_tx: Sender<RuntimeRequest>,
 139:     authoritative_identity: AuthoritativeTransportIdentity,
 140:     round_trip_in_flight: Arc<AtomicBool>,
 141:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 142:     test_state: RuntimeTestState,
 143: }

 200:     pub(crate) fn shutdown(&self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {

 202:         self.request(|reply| RuntimeRequest::Shutdown { reply })
 203:     }

 209:     pub(crate) fn set_exit_mode(&self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 210:         self.request(|reply| RuntimeRequest::SetExitMode { mode, reply })
 211:     }

 213:     pub(crate) fn authoritative_round_trip(
 214:         &self,
 215:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 216:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 217:         timeout: Duration,
 218:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportResponse, BackendError> {
 219:         self.acquire_round_trip_slot()?;
 220:         let result = self.request(|reply| RuntimeRequest::AuthoritativeRoundTrip {
 221:             remote_addr,
 222:             payload,
 223:             timeout,
 224:             reply,
 225:         });
 226:         self.round_trip_in_flight.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
 227:         result
 228:     }

 307: 
 308:     fn acquire_round_trip_slot(&self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 309:         self.round_trip_in_flight
 310:             .compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::SeqCst, Ordering::SeqCst)
 311:             .map(|_| ())
 312:             .map_err(|_| {
 313:                 BackendError::internal(
 314:                     "linux userspace-shared authoritative transport round trip rejected because another round trip is already in flight",
 315:                 )
 316:             })
 317:     }
 318: 
 319:     fn request<T>(
 320:         &self,
 321:         make_request: impl FnOnce(ReplySender<T>) -> RuntimeRequest,
 322:     ) -> Result<T, BackendError> {
 323:         let (reply_tx, reply_rx) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
 324:         self.command_tx.send(make_request(reply_tx)).map_err(|_| {
 325:             BackendError::internal("linux userspace-shared runtime worker is unavailable")
 326:         })?;
 327:         reply_rx.recv().map_err(|_| {
 328:             BackendError::internal("linux userspace-shared runtime worker dropped a reply")
 329:         })?
 330:     }
 331: }
 332: 
 333: #[derive(Debug)]
 334: enum RuntimeRequest {
 335:     ConfigurePeer {
 336:         peer: PeerConfig,
 337:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 338:     },
 339:     UpdatePeerEndpoint {
 340:         node_id: NodeId,
 341:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 342:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 343:     },
 344:     CurrentPeerEndpoint {
 345:         node_id: NodeId,
 346:         reply: ReplySender<Option<SocketEndpoint>>,
 347:     },
 348:     PeerLatestHandshake {
 349:         node_id: NodeId,
 350:         reply: ReplySender<Option<u64>>,
 351:     },
 352:     RemovePeer {
 353:         node_id: NodeId,
 354:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 355:     },
 356:     ApplyRoutes {
 357:         routes: Vec<Route>,
 358:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 359:     },
 360:     SetExitMode {
 361:         mode: ExitMode,
 362:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 363:     },
 364:     Stats {
 365:         reply: ReplySender<TunnelStats>,
 366:     },
 367:     InitiatePeerHandshake {
 368:         node_id: NodeId,
 369:         force_resend: bool,
 370:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 371:     },
 372:     AuthoritativeRoundTrip {
 373:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 374:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 375:         timeout: Duration,
 376:         reply: ReplySender<AuthoritativeTransportResponse>,
 377:     },
 378:     AuthoritativeSend {
 379:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 380:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 381:         reply: ReplySender<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity>,
 382:     },
 383:     Shutdown {
 384:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 385:     },
 386:     #[cfg(test)]
 387:     DebugWorkerLocalAddr {
 388:         reply: ReplySender<SocketAddr>,
 389:     },
 390:     #[cfg(test)]
 391:     DebugTransportGeneration {
 392:         reply: ReplySender<u64>,
 393:     },
 394:     #[cfg(test)]
 395:     DebugRecordedAuthoritativeOperations {
 396:         reply: ReplySender<Vec<RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation>>,
 397:     },
 398:     #[cfg(test)]
 399:     DebugRecordedPeerCiphertextIngress {
 400:         reply: ReplySender<Vec<RecordedPeerCiphertextIngress>>,
 401:     },
 402:     #[cfg(test)]
 403:     DebugRecordedPeerCiphertextEgress {
 404:         reply: ReplySender<Vec<RecordedPeerCiphertextEgress>>,
 405:     },
 406:     #[cfg(test)]
 407:     DebugInjectPlaintextPacket {
 408:         packet: Vec<u8>,
 409:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 410:     },
 411:     #[cfg(test)]
 412:     DebugRecordedTunnelPlaintextPackets {
 413:         reply: ReplySender<Vec<RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket>>,
 414:     },
 415:     #[cfg(test)]
 416:     DebugQueueTunPlaintextPacket {
 417:         packet: Vec<u8>,
 418:         reply: ReplySender<()>,
 419:     },
 420:     #[cfg(test)]
 421:     DebugRecordedTunOutboundPackets {
 422:         reply: ReplySender<Vec<Vec<u8>>>,
 423:     },
 424: }
 425: 
 426: #[derive(Debug)]
 427: struct RuntimeState {
 428:     context: RuntimeContext,
 429:     tun_device: TunDevice,
 430:     tun_lifecycle: SharedTunLifecycle,
 431:     authoritative_socket: AuthoritativeSocket,
 432:     engine: UserspaceEngine,
 433:     peers: BTreeMap<NodeId, PeerConfig>,
 434:     current_routes: Vec<Route>,
 435:     current_exit_mode: ExitMode,
 436:     outstanding_round_trip: Option<OutstandingRoundTripState>,
 437:     recorded_authoritative_operations: Vec<RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation>,
 438:     recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress: Vec<RecordedPeerCiphertextEgress>,
 439:     handshake_telemetry: HandshakeTelemetry,
 440: }
 441: 
 442: impl RuntimeState {
 443:     fn authoritative_identity(&self) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, BackendError> {
 444:         self.authoritative_socket
 445:             .identity(AUTHORITATIVE_TRANSPORT_LABEL)
 446:     }
 447: 
 448:     fn configure_peer(&mut self, peer: PeerConfig) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 449:         let disposition = self.engine.configure_peer(&peer)?;
 450:         let node_id = peer.node_id.clone();
 451:         self.peers.insert(node_id.clone(), peer);
 452:         if matches!(disposition, ConfigurePeerDisposition::Replaced) {
 453:             self.handshake_telemetry.clear_peer(&node_id);
 454:         }
 455:         Ok(())
 456:     }
 457: 
 458:     fn update_peer_endpoint(
 459:         &mut self,
 460:         node_id: &NodeId,
 461:         endpoint: SocketEndpoint,
 462:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 463:         let Some(peer) = self.peers.get_mut(node_id) else {
 464:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 465:         };
 466:         peer.endpoint = endpoint;
 467:         self.engine.update_peer_endpoint(node_id, endpoint)?;
 468:         Ok(())
 469:     }
 470: 
 471:     fn current_peer_endpoint(
 472:         &self,
 473:         node_id: &NodeId,
 474:     ) -> Result<Option<SocketEndpoint>, BackendError> {
 475:         Ok(self.engine.current_peer_endpoint(node_id))
 476:     }
 477: 
 478:     fn peer_latest_handshake_unix(&self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Result<Option<u64>, BackendError> {
 479:         if !self.engine.has_peer(node_id) {
 480:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input("peer is not configured"));
 481:         }
 482:         Ok(self.handshake_telemetry.latest_handshake(node_id))
 483:     }
 484: 
 485:     fn remove_peer(&mut self, node_id: &NodeId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 486:         if self.peers.remove(node_id).is_some() {
 487:             self.engine.remove_peer(node_id);
 488:             self.handshake_telemetry.clear_peer(node_id);
 489:         }
 490:         Ok(())
 491:     }
 492: 
 493:     fn stats(&self) -> TunnelStats {
 494:         let engine_stats = self.engine.stats();
 495:         TunnelStats {
 496:             peer_count: self.peers.len(),
 497:             bytes_tx: engine_stats.bytes_tx,
 498:             bytes_rx: engine_stats.bytes_rx,
 499:             using_relay_path: false,
 500:         }
 501:     }
 502: 
 503:     fn initiate_peer_handshake(
 504:         &mut self,
 505:         node_id: &NodeId,
 506:         force_resend: bool,
 507:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 508:         let outcome = self.engine.initiate_handshake(
 509:             node_id,
 510:             self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation(),
 511:             force_resend,
 512:         )?;
 513:         self.apply_engine_processing_outcome(outcome)
 514:     }
 515: 
 516:     fn apply_routes(&mut self, routes: Vec<Route>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 517:         self.tun_lifecycle.reconcile_routes(
 518:             &self.context.interface_name,
 519:             &self.current_routes,
 520:             &routes,
 521:         )?;
 522:         self.current_routes = routes;
 523:         Ok(())
 524:     }
 525: 
 526:     fn set_exit_mode(&mut self, mode: ExitMode) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 527:         self.tun_lifecycle
 528:             .reconcile_exit_mode(self.current_exit_mode, mode)?;
 529:         self.current_exit_mode = mode;
 530:         Ok(())
 531:     }
 532: 
 533:     fn start_authoritative_round_trip(
 534:         &mut self,
 535:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 536:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 537:         timeout: Duration,
 538:         reply: ReplySender<AuthoritativeTransportResponse>,
 539:     ) {
 540:         let error_reply = reply.clone();
 541:         let result = (|| -> Result<OutstandingRoundTripState, BackendError> {
 542:             if self.outstanding_round_trip.is_some() {
 543:                 return Err(BackendError::internal(
 544:                     "linux userspace-shared authoritative transport round trip rejected because another round trip is already in flight",
 545:                 ));
 546:             }
 547:             self.reject_round_trip_target(remote_addr)?;
 548: 
 549:             let local_addr = self.authoritative_socket.local_addr()?;
 550:             let transport_generation = self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation();
 551:             self.recorded_authoritative_operations
 552:                 .push(RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation {
 553:                     kind: RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::RoundTrip,
 554:                     local_addr,
 555:                     remote_addr,
 556:                     payload: payload.clone(),
 557:                     timeout: Some(timeout),
 558:                     transport_generation,
 559:                 });
 560:             self.authoritative_socket.send_to(remote_addr, &payload)?;
 561:             Ok(OutstandingRoundTripState {
 562:                 remote_addr,
 563:                 deadline: Instant::now()
 564:                     .checked_add(timeout)
 565:                     .unwrap_or_else(|| Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(24 * 60 * 60)),
 566:                 reply,
 567:                 transport_generation,
 568:             })
 569:         })();
 570: 
 571:         match result {
 572:             Ok(outstanding) => {
 573:                 self.outstanding_round_trip = Some(outstanding);
 574:             }
 575:             Err(err) => {
 576:                 let _ = error_reply.send(Err(err));
 577:             }
 578:         }
 579:     }
 580: 
 581:     fn authoritative_send(
 582:         &mut self,
 583:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 584:         payload: Vec<u8>,
 585:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, BackendError> {
 586:         let identity = self.authoritative_identity()?;
 587:         self.recorded_authoritative_operations
 588:             .push(RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation {
 589:                 kind: RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperationKind::Send,
 590:                 local_addr: identity.local_addr,
 591:                 remote_addr,
 592:                 payload: payload.clone(),
 593:                 timeout: None,
 594:                 transport_generation: self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation(),
 595:             });
 596:         self.authoritative_socket.send_to(remote_addr, &payload)?;
 597:         Ok(identity)
 598:     }
 599: 
 600:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 601:     fn recorded_authoritative_operations(&self) -> Vec<RecordedAuthoritativeTransportOperation> {
 602:         self.recorded_authoritative_operations.clone()
 603:     }
 604: 
 605:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 606:     fn recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress(&self) -> Vec<RecordedPeerCiphertextIngress> {
 607:         self.engine.recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress().to_vec()
 608:     }
 609: 
 610:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 611:     fn recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress(&self) -> Vec<RecordedPeerCiphertextEgress> {
 612:         self.recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress.clone()
 613:     }
 614: 
 615:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 616:     fn recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets(&self) -> Vec<RecordedTunnelPlaintextPacket> {
 617:         self.engine.recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets().to_vec()
 618:     }
 619: 
 620:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 621:     fn transport_generation(&self) -> u64 {
 622:         self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation()
 623:     }
 624: 
 625:     fn next_wait_timeout(&self) -> Duration {
 626:         let Some(outstanding) = self.outstanding_round_trip.as_ref() else {
 627:             return WORKER_POLL_INTERVAL;
 628:         };
 629:         let now = Instant::now();
 630:         if outstanding.deadline <= now {
 631:             Duration::ZERO
 632:         } else {
 633:             outstanding
 634:                 .deadline
 635:                 .saturating_duration_since(now)
 636:                 .min(WORKER_POLL_INTERVAL)
 637:         }
 638:     }
 639: 
 640:     fn poll_authoritative_socket(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 641:         while let Some(datagram) = self.authoritative_socket.try_recv()? {
 642:             if self.try_deliver_round_trip_response(&datagram)? {
 643:                 continue;
 644:             }
 645: 
 646:             let local_addr = self.authoritative_socket.local_addr()?;
 647:             let outcome = self.engine.process_inbound_ciphertext(
 648:                 datagram.remote_addr,
 649:                 local_addr,
 650:                 datagram.payload,
 651:                 self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation(),
 652:             )?;
 653:             self.apply_engine_processing_outcome(outcome)?;
 654:         }
 655:         Ok(())
 656:     }
 657: 
 658:     fn poll_tun_device(&mut self) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 659:         while let Some(packet) = self.tun_device.recv_packet()? {
 660:             let outcome = match self
 661:                 .engine
 662:                 .inject_plaintext_packet(&packet, self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation())
 663:             {
 664:                 Ok(outcome) => outcome,
 665:                 Err(err) if should_drop_tun_plaintext_packet_error(&err) => continue,
 666:                 Err(err) => return Err(err),
 667:             };
 668:             self.apply_engine_processing_outcome(outcome)?;
 669:         }
 670:         Ok(())
 671:     }
 672: 
 673:     fn expire_timed_out_round_trip(&mut self) {
 674:         let Some(outstanding) = self.outstanding_round_trip.as_ref() else {
 675:             return;
 676:         };
 677:         if Instant::now() < outstanding.deadline {
 678:             return;
 679:         }
 680:         let remote_addr = outstanding.remote_addr;
 681:         self.fail_outstanding_round_trip(BackendError::internal(format!(
 682:             "linux userspace-shared authoritative transport round trip to {remote_addr} timed out"
 683:         )));
 684:     }
 685: 
 686:     fn fail_outstanding_round_trip(&mut self, err: BackendError) {
 687:         if let Some(outstanding) = self.outstanding_round_trip.take() {
 688:             let _ = outstanding.reply.send(Err(err));
 689:         }
 690:     }
 691: 
 692:     fn reject_round_trip_target(&self, remote_addr: SocketAddr) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 693:         let matches_peer_endpoint = self.engine.has_endpoint(remote_addr);
 694:         if matches_peer_endpoint {
 695:             return Err(BackendError::invalid_input(
 696:                 "linux userspace-shared authoritative transport round trip target matches a configured peer endpoint",
 697:             ));
 698:         }
 699:         Ok(())
 700:     }
 701: 
 702:     fn try_deliver_round_trip_response(
 703:         &mut self,
 704:         datagram: &super::socket::ReceivedDatagram,
 705:     ) -> Result<bool, BackendError> {
 706:         let Some(outstanding) = self.outstanding_round_trip.as_ref() else {
 707:             return Ok(false);
 708:         };
 709:         if datagram.remote_addr != outstanding.remote_addr {
 710:             return Ok(false);
 711:         }
 712:         if self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation() != outstanding.transport_generation {
 713:             return Ok(false);
 714:         }
 715: 
 716:         let local_addr = self.authoritative_socket.local_addr()?;
 717:         let response = AuthoritativeTransportResponse {
 718:             local_addr,
 719:             remote_addr: datagram.remote_addr,
 720:             payload: datagram.payload.clone(),
 721:         };
 722:         let outstanding = self
 723:             .outstanding_round_trip
 724:             .take()
 725:             .expect("outstanding round trip should still exist");
 726:         let _ = outstanding.reply.send(Ok(response));
 727:         Ok(true)
 728:     }
 729: 
 730:     #[cfg_attr(not(test), allow(dead_code))]
 731:     fn inject_plaintext_packet_for_test(&mut self, packet: Vec<u8>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 732:         let outcome = self
 733:             .engine
 734:             .inject_plaintext_packet(&packet, self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation())?;
 735:         self.apply_engine_processing_outcome(outcome)
 736:     }
 737: 
 738:     #[cfg(test)]
 739:     fn queue_tun_plaintext_packet_for_test(&mut self, packet: Vec<u8>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 740:         self.tun_device.queue_inbound_packet_for_test(packet)
 741:     }
 742: 
 743:     #[cfg(test)]
 744:     fn recorded_tun_outbound_packets(&self) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, BackendError> {
 745:         self.tun_device.recorded_outbound_packets_for_test()
 746:     }
 747: 
 748:     fn apply_engine_processing_outcome(
 749:         &mut self,
 750:         outcome: super::engine::EngineProcessingOutcome,
 751:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
 752:         let local_addr = self.authoritative_socket.local_addr()?;
 753:         let transport_generation = self.authoritative_socket.transport_generation();
 754:         for packet in outcome.outbound_ciphertext_packets {
 755:             self.recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress
 756:                 .push(RecordedPeerCiphertextEgress {
 757:                     local_addr,
 758:                     remote_addr: packet.remote_addr,
 759:                     payload: packet.payload.clone(),
 760:                     transport_generation,
 761:                 });
 762:             self.authoritative_socket
 763:                 .send_to(packet.remote_addr, &packet.payload)?;
 764:         }
 765:         for packet in outcome.tunnel_plaintext_packets {
 766:             self.tun_device.send_packet(&packet)?;
 767:         }
 768:         if let Some((node_id, observed_unix)) = outcome.authenticated_handshake {
 769:             self.handshake_telemetry
 770:                 .record_authenticated_handshake(&node_id, observed_unix);
 771:         }
 772:         Ok(())
 773:     }
 774: }
 775: 
 776: fn should_drop_tun_plaintext_packet_error(err: &BackendError) -> bool {
 777:     err.kind == BackendErrorKind::InvalidInput
 778:         && matches!(
 779:             err.message.as_str(),
 780:             "plaintext packet does not contain a valid IPv4/IPv6 destination address"
 781:                 | "no configured peer allowed IP matches the plaintext packet destination"
 782:         )
 783: }
 784: 
 785: #[derive(Debug)]
 786: struct OutstandingRoundTripState {
 787:     remote_addr: SocketAddr,
 788:     deadline: Instant,
 789:     reply: ReplySender<AuthoritativeTransportResponse>,
 790:     transport_generation: u64,
 791: }
 792: 
 793: #[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
 794: struct RuntimeTestState {
 795:     worker_exit_count: Arc<AtomicUsize>,
 796: }
 797: 
 798: struct WorkerRuntimeParts {
 799:     context: RuntimeContext,
 800:     tun_device: TunDevice,
 801:     authoritative_socket: AuthoritativeSocket,
 802:     engine: UserspaceEngine,
 803:     tun_lifecycle: SharedTunLifecycle,
 804:     command_rx: Receiver<RuntimeRequest>,
 805:     ready_tx: ReplySender<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity>,
 806:     test_state: RuntimeTestState,
 807: }
 808: 
 809: fn run_worker(parts: WorkerRuntimeParts) {
 810:     let WorkerRuntimeParts {
 811:         context,
 812:         tun_device,
 813:         authoritative_socket,
 814:         engine,
 815:         tun_lifecycle,
 816:         command_rx,
 817:         ready_tx,
 818:         test_state,
 819:     } = parts;
 820:     let mut state = RuntimeState {
 821:         context,
 822:         tun_device,
 823:         tun_lifecycle,
 824:         authoritative_socket,
 825:         engine,
 826:         peers: BTreeMap::new(),
 827:         current_routes: Vec::new(),
 828:         current_exit_mode: ExitMode::Off,
 829:         outstanding_round_trip: None,
 830:         recorded_authoritative_operations: Vec::new(),
 831:         recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress: Vec::new(),
 832:         handshake_telemetry: HandshakeTelemetry::default(),
 833:     };
 834: 
 835:     match state.authoritative_identity() {
 836:         Ok(identity) => {
 837:             if ready_tx.send(Ok(identity)).is_err() {
 838:                 test_state.worker_exit_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
 839:                 return;
 840:             }
 841:         }
 842:         Err(err) => {
 843:             let _ = ready_tx.send(Err(err));
 844:             test_state.worker_exit_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
 845:             return;
 846:         }
 847:     }
 848: 
 849:     loop {
 850:         match command_rx.recv_timeout(state.next_wait_timeout()) {
 851:             Ok(request) => {
 852:                 if !handle_request(&mut state, request) {
 853:                     break;
 854:                 }
 855:                 loop {
 856:                     match command_rx.try_recv() {
 857:                         Ok(request) => {
 858:                             if !handle_request(&mut state, request) {
 859:                                 test_state.worker_exit_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
 860:                                 return;
 861:                             }
 862:                         }
 863:                         Err(TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
 864:                         Err(TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {
 865:                             state.fail_outstanding_round_trip(BackendError::internal(
 866:                                 "linux userspace-shared runtime worker command channel disconnected during authoritative transport processing",
 867:                             ));
 868:                             test_state.worker_exit_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
 869:                             return;
 870:                         }
 871:                     }
 872:                 }
 873:             }
 874:             Err(RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) => {}
 875:             Err(RecvTimeoutError::Disconnected) => break,
 876:         }
 877: 
 878:         if let Err(err) = state.poll_authoritative_socket() {
 879:             state.fail_outstanding_round_trip(err);
 880:             break;
 881:         }
 882:         if let Err(err) = state.poll_tun_device() {
 883:             state.fail_outstanding_round_trip(err);
 884:             break;
 885:         }
 886:         state.expire_timed_out_round_trip();
 887:     }
 888: 
 889:     state.fail_outstanding_round_trip(BackendError::internal(
 890:         "linux userspace-shared runtime worker exited while an authoritative transport round trip was still in flight",
 891:     ));
 892:     test_state.worker_exit_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
 893: }
 894: 
 895: fn handle_request(state: &mut RuntimeState, request: RuntimeRequest) -> bool {
 896:     match request {
 897:         RuntimeRequest::ConfigurePeer { peer, reply } => {
 898:             let _ = reply.send(state.configure_peer(peer));
 899:             true
 900:         }
 901:         RuntimeRequest::UpdatePeerEndpoint {
 902:             node_id,
 903:             endpoint,
 904:             reply,
 905:         } => {
 906:             let _ = reply.send(state.update_peer_endpoint(&node_id, endpoint));
 907:             true
 908:         }
 909:         RuntimeRequest::CurrentPeerEndpoint { node_id, reply } => {
 910:             let _ = reply.send(state.current_peer_endpoint(&node_id));
 911:             true
 912:         }
 913:         RuntimeRequest::PeerLatestHandshake { node_id, reply } => {
 914:             let _ = reply.send(state.peer_latest_handshake_unix(&node_id));
 915:             true
 916:         }
 917:         RuntimeRequest::RemovePeer { node_id, reply } => {
 918:             let _ = reply.send(state.remove_peer(&node_id));
 919:             true
 920:         }
 921:         RuntimeRequest::ApplyRoutes { routes, reply } => {
 922:             let _ = reply.send(state.apply_routes(routes));
 923:             true
 924:         }
 925:         RuntimeRequest::SetExitMode { mode, reply } => {
 926:             let _ = reply.send(state.set_exit_mode(mode));
 927:             true
 928:         }
 929:         RuntimeRequest::Stats { reply } => {
 930:             let _ = reply.send(Ok(state.stats()));
 931:             true
 932:         }
 933:         RuntimeRequest::InitiatePeerHandshake {
 934:             node_id,
 935:             force_resend,
 936:             reply,
 937:         } => {
 938:             let _ = reply.send(state.initiate_peer_handshake(&node_id, force_resend));
 939:             true
 940:         }
 941:         RuntimeRequest::AuthoritativeRoundTrip {
 942:             remote_addr,
 943:             payload,
 944:             timeout,
 945:             reply,
 946:         } => {
 947:             state.start_authoritative_round_trip(remote_addr, payload, timeout, reply);
 948:             true
 949:         }
 950:         RuntimeRequest::AuthoritativeSend {
 951:             remote_addr,
 952:             payload,
 953:             reply,
 954:         } => {
 955:             let _ = reply.send(state.authoritative_send(remote_addr, payload));
 956:             true
 957:         }
 958:         RuntimeRequest::Shutdown { reply } => {
 959:             state.fail_outstanding_round_trip(BackendError::internal(
 960:                 "linux userspace-shared authoritative transport round trip canceled during backend shutdown",
 961:             ));
 962:             let _ = reply.send(state.set_exit_mode(ExitMode::Off));
 963:             false
 964:         }
 965:         #[cfg(test)]
 966:         RuntimeRequest::DebugWorkerLocalAddr { reply } => {
 967:             let _ = reply.send(state.authoritative_socket.local_addr());
 968:             true
 969:         }
 970:         #[cfg(test)]
 971:         RuntimeRequest::DebugTransportGeneration { reply } => {
 972:             let _ = reply.send(Ok(state.transport_generation()));
 973:             true
 974:         }
 975:         #[cfg(test)]
 976:         RuntimeRequest::DebugRecordedAuthoritativeOperations { reply } => {
 977:             let _ = reply.send(Ok(state.recorded_authoritative_operations()));
 978:             true
 979:         }
 980:         #[cfg(test)]
 981:         RuntimeRequest::DebugRecordedPeerCiphertextIngress { reply } => {
 982:             let _ = reply.send(Ok(state.recorded_peer_ciphertext_ingress()));
 983:             true
 984:         }
 985:         #[cfg(test)]
 986:         RuntimeRequest::DebugRecordedPeerCiphertextEgress { reply } => {
 987:             let _ = reply.send(Ok(state.recorded_peer_ciphertext_egress()));
 988:             true
 989:         }
 990:         #[cfg(test)]
 991:         RuntimeRequest::DebugInjectPlaintextPacket { packet, reply } => {
 992:             let _ = reply.send(state.inject_plaintext_packet_for_test(packet));
 993:             true
 994:         }
 995:         #[cfg(test)]
 996:         RuntimeRequest::DebugRecordedTunnelPlaintextPackets { reply } => {
 997:             let _ = reply.send(Ok(state.recorded_tunnel_plaintext_packets()));
 998:             true
 999:         }
1000:         #[cfg(test)]
1001:         RuntimeRequest::DebugQueueTunPlaintextPacket { packet, reply } => {
1002:             let _ = reply.send(state.queue_tun_plaintext_packet_for_test(packet));
1003:             true
1004:         }
1005:         #[cfg(test)]
1006:         RuntimeRequest::DebugRecordedTunOutboundPackets { reply } => {
1007:             let _ = reply.send(state.recorded_tun_outbound_packets());
1008:             true
1009:         }
1010:     }
1011: }

===== /crates/rustynet-backend-wireguard/src/userspace_shared/socket.rs =====

   1: use std::io::ErrorKind;
   2: use std::net::{Ipv4Addr, SocketAddr, SocketAddrV4, UdpSocket};
   3: use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
   4: 
   5: use rustynet_backend_api::{AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, BackendError};
   6: 
   7: pub(crate) const AUTHORITATIVE_TRANSPORT_LABEL: &str =
   8:     "wireguard-linux-userspace-shared-authoritative-transport";
   9: 
  10: static NEXT_TRANSPORT_GENERATION: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(1);
  11: 
  12: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  13: pub(crate) struct ReceivedDatagram {
  14:     pub(crate) remote_addr: SocketAddr,
  15:     pub(crate) payload: Vec<u8>,
  16: }
  17: 
  18: #[derive(Debug)]
  19: pub(crate) struct AuthoritativeSocket {
  20:     socket: UdpSocket,
  21:     transport_generation: u64,
  22: }
  23: 
  24: impl AuthoritativeSocket {
  25:     pub(crate) fn bind(listen_port: u16) -> Result<Self, BackendError> {
  26:         let bind_addr = SocketAddr::V4(SocketAddrV4::new(Ipv4Addr::UNSPECIFIED, listen_port));
  27:         let socket = UdpSocket::bind(bind_addr).map_err(|err| {
  28:             BackendError::internal(format!(
  29:                 "linux userspace-shared authoritative UDP socket bind failed on {bind_addr}: {err}"
  30:             ))
  31:         })?;
  32:         socket.set_nonblocking(true).map_err(|err| {
  33:             BackendError::internal(format!(
  34:                 "linux userspace-shared authoritative UDP socket nonblocking setup failed on {bind_addr}: {err}"
  35:             ))
  36:         })?;
  37:         Ok(Self {
  38:             socket,
  39:             transport_generation: NEXT_TRANSPORT_GENERATION.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst),
  40:         })
  41:     }
  42: 
  43:     pub(crate) fn local_addr(&self) -> Result<SocketAddr, BackendError> {
  44:         self.socket.local_addr().map_err(|err| {
  45:             BackendError::internal(format!(
  46:                 "linux userspace-shared authoritative UDP socket local_addr failed: {err}"
  47:             ))
  48:         })
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     pub(crate) fn identity(
  52:         &self,
  53:         label: &'static str,
  54:     ) -> Result<AuthoritativeTransportIdentity, BackendError> {
  55:         Ok(AuthoritativeTransportIdentity {
  56:             local_addr: self.local_addr()?,
  57:             label: label.to_string(),
  58:         })
  59:     }
  60: 
  61:     pub(crate) fn transport_generation(&self) -> u64 {
  62:         self.transport_generation
  63:     }
  64: 
  65:     pub(crate) fn send_to(
  66:         &self,
  67:         remote_addr: SocketAddr,
  68:         payload: &[u8],
  69:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  70:         self.socket.send_to(payload, remote_addr).map_err(|err| {
  71:             BackendError::internal(format!(
  72:                 "linux userspace-shared authoritative UDP socket send_to failed for {remote_addr}: {err}"
  73:             ))
  74:         })?;
  75:         Ok(())
  76:     }
  77: 
  78:     pub(crate) fn try_recv(&self) -> Result<Option<ReceivedDatagram>, BackendError> {
  79:         let mut buffer = vec![0u8; 65_535];
  80:         match self.socket.recv_from(&mut buffer) {
  81:             Ok((len, remote_addr)) => {
  82:                 buffer.truncate(len);
  83:                 Ok(Some(ReceivedDatagram {
  84:                     remote_addr,
  85:                     payload: buffer,
  86:                 }))
  87:             }
  88:             Err(err) if matches!(err.kind(), ErrorKind::WouldBlock | ErrorKind::TimedOut) => {
  89:                 Ok(None)
  90:             }
  91:             Err(err) => Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
  92:                 "linux userspace-shared authoritative UDP socket recv_from failed: {err}"
  93:             ))),
  94:         }
  95:     }
  96: }

===== /crates/rustynet-backend-wireguard/src/userspace_shared/tun.rs =====

   1: use std::collections::VecDeque;
   2: use std::fmt;
   3: use std::io::ErrorKind;
   4: use std::net::Ipv4Addr;
   5: use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
   6: 
   7: use rustynet_backend_api::{BackendError, ExitMode, Route, RouteKind, RuntimeContext};
   8: use rustynet_tun::SyncDevice;
   9: 
  10: use crate::linux_command::{LinuxCommandRunner, WireguardCommandRunner, validate_interface_name};
  11: 
  12: pub(crate) struct TunDevice {
  13:     _inner: TunDeviceInner,
  14: }
  15: 
  16: impl fmt::Debug for TunDevice {
  17:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
  18:         f.write_str("TunDevice(..)")
  19:     }
  20: }
  21: 
  22: impl TunDevice {
  23:     fn real(device: SyncDevice) -> Self {
  24:         Self {
  25:             _inner: TunDeviceInner::Real(device),
  26:         }
  27:     }
  28: 
  29:     pub(crate) fn test_handle(state: TunTestState) -> Self {
  30:         Self {
  31:             _inner: TunDeviceInner::Test(TestTunDeviceHandle::new(state)),
  32:         }
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     pub(crate) fn recv_packet(&self) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, BackendError> {
  36:         match &self._inner {
  37:             TunDeviceInner::Real(device) => {
  38:                 let mut buffer = vec![0u8; 65_535];
  39:                 match device.recv(&mut buffer) {
  40:                     Ok(len) => {
  41:                         buffer.truncate(len);
  42:                         Ok(Some(buffer))
  43:                     }
  44:                     Err(err)
  45:                         if matches!(err.kind(), ErrorKind::WouldBlock | ErrorKind::TimedOut) =>
  46:                     {
  47:                         Ok(None)
  48:                     }
  49:                     Err(err) => Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
  50:                         "linux userspace-shared TUN receive failed: {err}"
  51:                     ))),
  52:                 }
  53:             }
  54:             TunDeviceInner::Test(handle) => {
  55:                 if let Some(message) = handle.state.take_next_recv_error() {
  56:                     return Err(BackendError::internal(message));
  57:                 }
  58:                 Ok(handle.dequeue_inbound_packet())
  59:             }
  60:         }
  61:     }
  62: 
  63:     pub(crate) fn send_packet(&self, packet: &[u8]) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  64:         match &self._inner {
  65:             TunDeviceInner::Real(device) => {
  66:                 let written = device.send(packet).map_err(|err| {
  67:                     BackendError::internal(format!("linux userspace-shared TUN send failed: {err}"))
  68:                 })?;
  69:                 if written != packet.len() {
  70:                     return Err(BackendError::internal(format!(
  71:                         "linux userspace-shared TUN send truncated packet: wrote {written} of {} bytes",
  72:                         packet.len()
  73:                     )));
  74:                 }
  75:                 Ok(())
  76:             }
  77:             TunDeviceInner::Test(handle) => {
  78:                 handle.record_outbound_packet(packet.to_vec());
  79:                 Ok(())
  80:             }
  81:         }
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     #[cfg(test)]
  85:     pub(crate) fn queue_inbound_packet_for_test(
  86:         &self,
  87:         packet: Vec<u8>,
  88:     ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
  89:         match &self._inner {
  90:             TunDeviceInner::Real(_) => Err(BackendError::internal(
  91:                 "real TUN device does not support test packet injection",
  92:             )),
  93:             TunDeviceInner::Test(handle) => {
  94:                 handle.queue_inbound_packet(packet);
  95:                 Ok(())
  96:             }
  97:         }
  98:     }
  99: 
 100:     #[cfg(test)]
 101:     pub(crate) fn recorded_outbound_packets_for_test(&self) -> Result<Vec<Vec<u8>>, BackendError> {
 102:         match &self._inner {
 103:             TunDeviceInner::Real(_) => Err(BackendError::internal(
 104:                 "real TUN device does not expose recorded outbound packets",
 105:             )),
 106:             TunDeviceInner::Test(handle) => Ok(handle.recorded_outbound_packets()),
 107:         }
 108:     }
 109: }
