 149: #[test]
 150: fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_lifecycle_exposes_authoritative_identity_only_while_running() {
 151:     let private_key_path = write_private_key([17; 32]);
 152:     let listen_port = free_listen_port();
 153:     let expected_local_addr = SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port));
 154:     let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
 155:         "rustynet0",
 156:         private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
 157:         listen_port,
 158:     )
 159:     .expect("backend should construct");
 160: 
 161:     assert!(backend.authoritative_transport_identity().is_none());
 162: 
 163:     backend
 164:         .start(runtime_context())
 165:         .expect("backend should start successfully");
 166: 
 167:     let identity = backend
 168:         .authoritative_transport_identity()
 169:         .expect("identity should exist after start");
 170:     assert_eq!(identity.local_addr, expected_local_addr);
 171: 
 172:     let second_start_err = backend
 173:         .start(runtime_context())
 174:         .expect_err("second start should fail");
 175:     assert_eq!(second_start_err.kind, BackendErrorKind::AlreadyRunning);
 176: 
 177:     backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
 178:     assert!(backend.authoritative_transport_identity().is_none());
 179: 
 180:     let rebound = UdpSocket::bind(SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], listen_port)))
 181:         .expect("shutdown should release the authoritative UDP socket");
 182:     drop(rebound);
 183: 
 184:     let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
 185: }

 187: #[test]
 188: fn linux_userspace_shared_backend_supports_route_and_exit_mode_lifecycle() {
 189:     let private_key_path = write_private_key([18; 32]);
 190:     let listen_port = free_listen_port();
 191:     let mut backend = LinuxUserspaceSharedBackend::new_for_test(
 192:         "rustynet0",
 193:         private_key_path.to_string_lossy(),
 194:         listen_port,
 195:     )
 196:     .expect("backend should construct");
 197: 
 198:     backend
 199:         .start(runtime_context())
 200:         .expect("backend should start successfully");
 201:     backend
 202:         .configure_peer(sample_peer("peer-a"))
 203:         .expect("peer should configure");
 204:     backend
 205:         .apply_routes(vec![Route {
 206:             destination_cidr: "100.64.20.0/24".to_string(),
 207:             via_node: NodeId::new("peer-a").expect("valid node id"),
 208:             kind: RouteKind::ExitNodeLan,
 209:         }])
 210:         .expect("route apply should succeed");
 211:     backend
 212:         .set_exit_mode(ExitMode::FullTunnel)
 213:         .expect("exit mode switch should succeed");
 214: 
 215:     let capabilities = backend.capabilities();
 216:     assert!(capabilities.supports_exit_nodes);
 217:     assert!(capabilities.supports_lan_routes);
 218: 
 219:     backend.shutdown().expect("shutdown should succeed");
 220:     let _ = fs::remove_file(private_key_path);
 221: }

 223: #[test]
 224: fn command_only_linux_backend_blocker_remains_unchanged() {
 225:     let backend = LinuxWireguardBackend::new(StubRunner, "rustynet0", "/tmp/wg.key", 51820)
 226:         .expect("backend should construct");
 227: 
 228:     assert_eq!(
 229:         backend.transport_socket_identity_blocker(),
 230:         Some(
 231:             "linux wireguard backend is a command-only adapter over an OS-managed WireGuard UDP socket; it exposes configuration and handshake queries but no authoritative packet-I/O handle or backend-owned datagram multiplexer, so the daemon cannot safely run STUN or relay bootstrap/refresh on the real peer-traffic transport, and a same-port daemon side socket is not authoritative transport identity".to_string()
 232:         )
 233:     );
 234: }

 237: fn command_only_macos_backend_blocker_remains_unchanged() {
 238:     let backend = MacosWireguardBackend::new(StubRunner, "utun9", "/tmp/wg.key", "en0", 51820)
 239:         .expect("backend should construct");
 240: 
 241:     assert_eq!(
 242:         backend.transport_socket_identity_blocker(),
 243:         Some(
 244:             "macos wireguard backend is a command-only adapter over wireguard-go and its OS-managed UDP socket; it exposes configuration and handshake queries but no authoritative packet-I/O handle or backend-owned datagram multiplexer, so the daemon cannot safely run STUN or relay bootstrap/refresh on the real peer-traffic transport, and a same-port daemon side socket is not authoritative transport identity".to_string()
 245:         )
 246:     );
 247: }

 249: #[test]
 250: fn command_only_windows_backend_blocker_remains_explicit() {
 251:     let backend = WindowsWireguardBackend::new(
 252:         StubRunner,
 253:         "rustynet0",
 254:         "/tmp/rustynet0.conf.dpapi",
 255:         "/tmp/wg.key",
 256:         "/tmp/wireguard.exe",
 257:         "/tmp/wg.exe",
 258:         "/tmp/netsh.exe",
 259:         51820,
 260:     )
 261:     .expect("windows backend should construct");
 262: 
 263:     assert_eq!(
 264:         backend.transport_socket_identity_blocker(),
 265:         Some(
 266:             "windows wireguard backend is a command-only adapter over the official WireGuard for Windows tunnel service and its OS-managed WireGuardNT UDP socket; it exposes configuration and handshake queries but no authoritative packet-I/O handle or backend-owned datagram multiplexer, so the daemon cannot safely run STUN or relay bootstrap/refresh on the real peer-traffic transport, and a same-port daemon side socket is not authoritative transport identity".to_string()
 267:         )
 268:     );
 269: }
